 458  class Sessions(unittest.TestCase):
 459      def setUp(self):
 460          self.saved = b5.gate, b5.run_arm, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b5.repro.agents, b5.cr.wait_cores
 461          self.ran = []
 462          b4.reading = lambda **kw: test_b4.good()
 463          b4.cores_ok = lambda: True
 464          b5.repro.agents = lambda: ''
 465          b5.cr.wait_cores = lambda label: None
 466          b5.gate = lambda ref, label: {'passed': True}
 467  
 468      def tearDown(self):
 469          b5.gate, b5.run_arm, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b5.repro.agents, b5.cr.wait_cores = self.saved
 470  
 471      def fake_arms(self, labels, battery_stop=False):
 472          def run_arm(k, out, ctx, emit):
 473              self.ran.append(k)
 474              return {'arm': k, 'label': labels.get(k, 'VALID'), 'problems': [], 'battery_stop': battery_stop}, []
 475          b5.run_arm = run_arm
 476  
 477      def session(self, out, smoke=False):
 478          with open(out / 'arms.jsonl', 'a') as alog, unittest.mock.patch('sys.stdout', io.StringIO()):
 479              return b5.session_loop(out, {'start': {'skin': 30.0}}, {'smoke': smoke, 'session': 1}, lambda r: None, alog)
 480  
 481      def test_order_invalid_stops_and_resume_redoes_it(self):
 482          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 483              out = Path(d)
 484              self.fake_arms({'A': 'INVALID'})
 485              self.assertIn('arm A INVALID', self.session(out))
 486              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['A'])
 487              self.fake_arms({})
 488              self.assertEqual(self.session(out), 'all arms done')
 489              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['A', 'A', 'B'])
 490  
 491      def test_smoke_gates_only_the_first_arm(self):
 492          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 493              self.fake_arms({k: b5.SMOKE_LABEL for k in b5.ARMS})
 494              gated = []
 495              b5.gate = lambda ref, label: gated.append(label) or {'passed': True}
 496              b5.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 497              self.assertEqual(self.session(Path(d), smoke=True), 'all arms done')
 498              self.assertEqual((self.ran, gated), (['A', 'B'], ['arm A']))
 499  
 500      def test_timed_gates_every_arm_and_safety_before_the_arm(self):
 501          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 502              self.fake_arms({})
 503              gated = []
 504              b5.gate = lambda ref, label: gated.append(label) or {'passed': True}
 505              self.assertEqual(self.session(Path(d)), 'all arms done')
 506              self.assertEqual(gated, ['arm A', 'arm B'])
 507          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 508              self.ran.clear()
 509              b4.reading = lambda **kw: test_b4.good(battery=29)
 510              self.assertIn('stopped before arm A', self.session(Path(d)))
 511              b4.reading = lambda **kw: test_b4.good()
 512              b5.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 513              self.assertIn('agent processes running', self.session(Path(d)))
 514              self.assertEqual(self.ran, [])
 515  
 516      def test_battery_stop_in_an_arm_ends_the_session(self):
 517          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 518              self.fake_arms({'A': 'INVALID'}, battery_stop=True)
 519              self.assertIn('battery below 30 %', self.session(Path(d)))
 520              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['A'])
 521  
 522      def test_definition_and_resume_refusal(self):
 523          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 524              old = b5.definition(Path(d))
 525              self.assertIn('benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh', old['files'])
 526              self.assertIn('benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/runs/20261102T143251Z/requests.jsonl', old['files'])
 527              self.assertIn('heat_control.py', old['files'])
 528              self.assertIn('--slot-save-path', old['commands']['server'])
 529              new = json.loads(json.dumps(old))
 530              new['files']['heat_control.py'] = '0' * 64
 531              self.assertEqual(len(b4.definition_diff(old, new)), 1)
 532  
 533      def test_battery_first_start_85_resume_50_smoke_none(self):
 534          self.assertIsNone(b5.battery_refusal(85, False, None))
 535          self.assertIn('< 85 %', b5.battery_refusal(84, False, None))
 536          self.assertIsNone(b5.battery_refusal(50, False, '20261110T000000Z'))
 537          self.assertIn('< 50 %', b5.battery_refusal(49, False, '20261110T000000Z'))
 538          self.assertIsNone(b5.battery_refusal(40, True, None))
 539  
 540      def test_smoke_thresholds_lower_the_deployed_constants_at_runtime(self):
 541          saved = hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C
 542          try:
 543              t = b5.smoke_thresholds(31.24)  # the arm-start skin
 544              self.assertEqual((t['pause_c'], t['resume_c'], t['min_active_s'], t['min_pause_s']), (32.24, 31.74, 60, 30))
 545              st = {'phase': 'ACTIVE', 'since': 0.0}
 546              now = 100.0
 547              rd = {'dump': {'t': now, 'status': 0, 'skin': 32.3}, 'fast': [{'t': now, 'cpu_c': 50.0, 'bat_c': 30.0}]}
 548              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(now, rd, st)[0], 'PAUSE')
 549              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(59.0, {**rd, 'dump': {**rd['dump'], 't': 59.0},
 550                                                'fast': [{'t': 59.0, 'cpu_c': 50.0, 'bat_c': 30.0}]}, st)[0], 'ACTIVE')
 551              rd['dump']['skin'] = 31.8
 552              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(now, rd, {'phase': 'PAUSE', 'since': 60.0})[0], 'PAUSE')
 553              rd['dump']['skin'] = 31.74
 554              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(now, rd, {'phase': 'PAUSE', 'since': 60.0})[0], 'ACTIVE')
 555          finally:
 556              hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C = saved
 557          self.assertEqual((hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C), (37.0, 35.0))
 558  
 559      def test_smoke_report_says_whether_a_pause_happened(self):
 560          run = {'utc': 'U', 'smoke': True, 'work_s': 180, 'git_head': 'h', 'start': test_b4.good(),
 561                 'definition': {'files': {}, 'commands': {'server': 'srv'}}}
 562          rec = {**arm_record(label=b5.SMOKE_LABEL, session=1, problems=[], heat_thresholds={'pause_c': 31.0}),
 563                 'metrics': {}}
 564          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 565              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, [{**CYC, 't': 0.0, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 140}], {}, Path(d))
 566          text = b5.report(run, [rec])
 567          self.assertIn('SMOKE HEAT THRESHOLDS', text)
 568          self.assertIn('NO PAUSE HAPPENED', text)
 569          self.assertIn("{'pause_c': 31.0}", text)
 570          rows = [{**CYC, 't': 0.0, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 140},
 571                  {'kind': 'heat', 'from': 'ACTIVE', 'to': 'PAUSE', 't': 61.0}]
 572          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 573              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, rows, {}, Path(d))
 574          text = b5.report(run, [rec])
 575          self.assertIn('1 pause(s) with camera stop, 0 resume(s)', text)
 576          self.assertIn('NO RESUME HAPPENED', text)
 577          rows.append({'kind': 'heat', 'from': 'PAUSE', 'to': 'ACTIVE', 't': 95.0})
 578          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 579              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, rows, {}, Path(d))
 580          text = b5.report(run, [rec])
 581          self.assertIn('1 pause(s) with camera stop, 1 resume(s)', text)
 582          self.assertNotIn('HAPPENED', text)
 583  
 584      def test_motors_and_serial_never_imported(self):
 585          self.assertNotIn('motors', sys.modules)
 586          self.assertNotIn('serial', sys.modules)
 587  
 588  
 589  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- one arm, real time, everything faked
 590  
 591  class FakeMonitor:
 592      """heat_control.HeatMonitor's interface; skin follows a plan by seconds since start()."""
 593      plan = [(0, 30.0)]
 594  
 595      def __init__(self):
 596          self.on_dump, self.t0, self.stopped = None, None, False
 597  
 598      def start(self):
 599          self.t0 = time.monotonic()
 600  
 601      def stop(self):
 602          self.stopped = True
 603  
 604      def readings(self):
 605          now = time.monotonic()
 606          skin = [s for at, s in self.plan if now - self.t0 >= at][-1]
 607          return {'dump': {'t': now, 'status': 0, 'skin': skin}, 'fast': [{'t': now, 'cpu_c': 50.0, 'bat_c': 30.0}]}
 608  
 609  
 610  class Watcher:
 611      def __init__(self, targets):
 612          self.targets = targets
 613  
 614      def start(self):
 615          return self
 616  
 617      def stop(self):
 618          return test_b4.watch()
 619  
 620  
 621  class RealTimeArm(unittest.TestCase):
 622      def setUp(self):
 623          self.patches = [unittest.mock.patch.object(*p) for p in [
 624              (b5, 'OFFER_S', 0.2), (b5, 'TICK_S', 0.5), (b5, 'POLL_S', 0.01), (main, 'CYCLE_MOVE_TIME', 0.05),
 625              (hc, 'MIN_ACTIVE_S', 0.6), (hc, 'MIN_PAUSE_S', 0.4), (hc, 'HeatMonitor', FakeMonitor),
 626              (hc, 'SKIN_PAUSE_C', hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C), (hc, 'SKIN_RESUME_C', hc.SKIN_RESUME_C),  # restored after
 627              (main, 'get_detector', lambda pinned: None), (b4, 'server_command', lambda out, k: ['srv']),
 628              (b4, 'start_server', lambda out, name, cmd: (unittest.mock.Mock(pid=4242), None, 0.1)),
 629              (b4, 'stop_server', lambda proc, log: (0, False)), (b4, 'warm', lambda prefix: {'save': {}}),
 630              (b5, 'picker_setup', lambda test: ({('V3', 'FWD', it['id']): ('t', [1], {}) for it in ITEMS}, [1])),
 631              (b4, 'reading', lambda pids=None, light=False, tag='': {**test_b4.good(
 632                  mem=('runner', 'llama_server', 'robotcam')), 'mono': time.monotonic()}),
 633              (b4, 'Watch', Watcher), (b4, 'cores_ok', lambda: True), (b5.repro, 'agents', lambda: ''),
 634              (b4, 'slot', lambda action: {'action': action, 'wall_s': 0.0}),
 635              (b5.b2, 'score', self.score), (b5.b2, 'preflight', lambda post, s, blocks, info, tag: {
 636                  ('V3', 'FWD', it['id']): ('t', [1], {}) for it in s['items']}),
 637              (main, 'Robot', lambda motors: self.robot)]]
 638          for p in self.patches:
 639              p.start()
 640          self.robot = FakeRobot(test_b4.FakeClock(), {})
 641          self.robot.clock.sleep = lambda s: time.sleep(0.01)
 642          self.saved_read = main.read_frame
 643          main.read_frame = self.robot.read
 644          self.decisions = []
 645  
 646      def tearDown(self):
 647          for p in self.patches:
 648              p.stop()
 649          main.read_frame = self.saved_read
 650  
 651      def score(self, post, it, v, order, prompt):
 652          self.decisions.append((time.monotonic(), it['id']))
 653          for _ in range(2):  # V3: reason, then the action
 654              time.sleep(0.045)
 655              post('/completion', {})
 656          return {'top': 'explore', 'reason': 'r', 'reason_tokens': 3}
 657  
 658      def run_arm(self, arm, skin_plan, smoke=False):
 659          FakeMonitor.plan = skin_plan
 660          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, unittest.mock.patch('sys.stdout', io.StringIO()), \
 661                  unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'post', lambda path, body: {'timings': {}}):
 662              ctx = {'session': 1, 'smoke': smoke, 'dur': 4.0, 'test': {'missions': {'M1': 'm'}, 'items': ITEMS},
 663                     'record': {i: 'explore' for i in range(1, 41)}}
 664              rows = []
 665              rec, rows = b5.run_arm(arm, Path(d), ctx, rows.append)
 666              trace = (Path(d) / rec['heat_trace']).read_text()
 667          return rec, rows, trace
 668  
 669      def check_common(self, rec, rows, label='VALID'):
 670          self.assertEqual(rec['problems'], [])
 671          self.assertEqual(rec['label'], label)
 672          x = rec['metrics']
 673          self.assertEqual(x['events']['scripted']['offered'], 20)
 674          for s, f in x['events'].items():
 675              self.assertEqual(f['offered'], sum(v for k, v in f.items() if k != 'offered'), (s, f))
 676          self.assertEqual(x['decisions_served'], sum(f['served'] for f in x['events'].values()))
 677          self.assertGreater(x['decisions_served'], 0)
 678          self.assertEqual(x['agreement']['ids_differ'], [])
 679          self.assertLessEqual(rec['overrun_s'], 1.0)
 680          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']][0], 'start')
 681          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']][-1], 'end')
 682          run = {'utc': 'U', 'smoke': False, 'work_s': 4, 'git_head': 'h', 'start': test_b4.good(),
 683                 'definition': {'files': {}, 'commands': {'server': 'srv'}}}
 684          text = b5.report(run, json.loads(json.dumps([rec])))  # as read back from arms.jsonl
 685          self.assertIn(f'== {rec["arm"]}: {label}', text)
 686          self.assertIn('agreement with B2 run of record', text)
 687  
 688      def test_arm_a_with_a_heat_pause(self):
 689          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('A', [(0, 30.0), (0.7, 38.0), (1.5, 34.0)])
 690          self.check_common(rec, rows)
 691          x = rec['metrics']
 692          self.assertEqual(x['pauses'], 1)
 693          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']], ['start', 'pause_start', 'pause_end', 'end'])
 694          heat = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat']
 695          t_off, t_on = heat[0]['t'], heat[1]['t']
 696          dec = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision']
 697          self.assertFalse([r for r in dec if t_off + 0.05 < r['started'] < t_on], 'a decision started in the pause')
 698          self.assertLessEqual(len(dec), 9)  # one per 0.5 s mark at most
 699          self.assertIn('"camera_stop_ok"', trace)
 700          self.assertEqual((self.robot.stops, self.robot.starts), (2, 2))  # pause + end; arm start + resume
 701  
 702      def test_smoke_thresholds_from_the_arm_start_skin_pause_and_resume(self):
 703          # the fake start reading's skin is 30.0: pause at 31.0, resume at 30.5; the deployed 37/35 never trigger here
 704          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('B', [(0, 30.0), (0.7, 31.2), (1.5, 30.4)], smoke=True)
 705          self.check_common(rec, rows, b5.SMOKE_LABEL)
 706          self.assertEqual((rec['heat_thresholds']['pause_c'], rec['heat_thresholds']['resume_c']), (31.0, 30.5))
 707          self.assertEqual((rec['metrics']['pauses'], rec['metrics']['resumes']), (1, 1))
 708          self.assertEqual((self.robot.stops, self.robot.starts), (2, 2))
 709          self.assertIn('"camera_start_ok"', trace)
 710  
 711      def test_timed_arm_keeps_the_deployed_thresholds(self):
 712          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('B', [(0, 30.0), (0.7, 31.2), (1.5, 30.4)])
 713          self.assertNotIn('heat_thresholds', rec)
 714          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['pauses'], 0)
 715          self.assertEqual((hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C), (37.0, 35.0))
 716  
 717      def test_arm_b_skin_above_36_holds_events(self):
 718          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('B', [(0, 30.0), (2.0, 36.5)])
 719          self.check_common(rec, rows)
 720          dec = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision' and r['fate'] == 'served']
 721          self.assertFalse([r for r in dec if r['started'] > 2.1], 'a decision started at skin >= 36')
 722          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['pauses'], 0)
 723          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['events']['scripted']['unserved'], 1)
 724          early = [r for r in dec if r['offered'] < 1.8]
 725          self.assertTrue(early and max(r['started'] - r['offered'] for r in early) < 0.15)
 726  
 727  
 728  if __name__ == '__main__':
 729      unittest.main(verbosity=1)
END

D3 BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/README.md (60 lines)
   1  # MIND-B5 — 30-minute motors-off mission: sustainable picker rate with camera, YOLO and heat pauses
   2  
   3  Research only. With RobotCam + YOLO running (CAM1-MID, #136) and the deployed adaptive heat pause in force (37 °C
   4  pause / 35 °C resume, #135), how many B2 V3 picker decisions does the phone serve in 30 minutes, how fast after an
   5  event, how much time do heat pauses take, and does throttling change the picker's choices? Timings count only from
   6  the owner's `oneshot.sh` run in native Termux with no agent resident.
   7  
   8  - **Motors off.** `motors.py` and pyserial are never imported (`main.py` imports neither; `run_mission.py`, which
   9    imports `motors` without `--dry`, is not imported); `main.Robot(None)` has no motors (distance 999 cm, moves are
  10    sleeps). An arm refuses to start, and is INVALID, if `motors` or `serial` is in `sys.modules` at its start or end.
  11    No serial port, no ESP32, no MTP, no memory notes, no vision calls.
  12  - **Setup per arm (not timed).** `main.get_detector(pinned=True)` first (run_mission's order); B4's server
  13    (`b4.server_command(<run>, 'P')`: b11716, setup_q4 flags, `--slot-save-path <run>/slots`, Q8_0 projector at 70
  14    image tokens; `bc.PINS`); the 40 B2 TEST prompts (V3 FWD, `b2.preflight`), the common prefix sent once and slot 0
  15    saved (`b4.warm`); `heat_control.HeatControl(HeatMonitor())` started (its own 1 s / 5 s root readings: essential
  16    safety monitoring; trace in the run folder); `robot.start_camera(heat)`; start reading; B4's 30 s watcher.
  17  - **Mission loop** = `Robot.run_mission` motors-off: heat transitions exactly as `main.py` (picker forbidden, camera
  18    stopped and shown stopped, `heat.enter`; resume: `start_camera(heat)`, `heat.enter('ACTIVE', since=before the
  19    start)`); HOLD sleeps 1 s, PAUSE the move time; ACTIVE runs B4's camera cycle (new frame, SizePolicy, detect) then
  20    the move time. Picker allowed only while ACTIVE and Android status < SEVERE; a forbid cancels a running decision
  21    before its next HTTP request, for good. Heat END, a camera start/stop failure or an unusable frame ends the arm.
  22  - **Events.** Scripted: the 40 B2 TEST items in id order, cycled, one every 10 s (180 per arm). Real: the most
  23    confident person's area bucket per ACTIVE cycle; appear / leave / bucket change → an event line in the B2 state
  24    format (mission M1, current activity exploring, memory none, all five actions; served, unscored). Pending queue of
  25    one: a newer event replaces an unserved one (dropped).
  26  - **Arms** (30 min of mission time each, order A then B): **A FIXED** at each 30 s mark, if allowed and idle, decide
  27    on the pending event; **B ADAPTIVE** as soon as an event is pending, the previous decision finished, the picker is
  28    allowed and the heat control's latest VIRTUAL-SKIN < 36.0 °C. A decision: slot 0 restored before every 5th, then
  29    `b2.score` unchanged (a real event's prompt is rendered first, inside its wall). A cancelled decision's event goes
  30    back to pending unless a newer one is waiting. The scheduler polls every 0.1 s (latency resolution).
  31  - **Deadline.** Nothing starts after it; the decision or camera cycle in flight finishes; overrun ≤ 15 s.
  32  - **Readings** (`b4.reading`, per-PID memory checked): start, end, one at each heat-pause start (after the camera
  33    stop) and end (after the camera start). RobotCam memory required while the camera is on.
  34  - **Gates.** Before every arm: cores 4–7, agent check, MEM-1 skin gate (launch-start skin + 1.0 °C, wait ≤ 30 min),
  35    status < 3, battery < 45 °C and ≥ 30 %. A timed run needs battery ≥ 85 % at its first start and ≥ 50 % at each
  36    `--resume`. Battery < 30 % at any reading ends the arm (INVALID) and the session.
  37  - **Labels.** Arm VALID / INVALID by B4's rules (watcher hit, cores, missing or incomplete reading, camera failure,
  38    agent, request errors, overrun > 15 s) plus heat END and battery stop; an INVALID arm ends the session and is redone
  39    with `--resume`. A vs B descriptive (no matching claim). Energy INDICATIVE.
  40  - **Metrics per arm:** decisions served; offered / served / dropped / cancelled / unserved (scripted and real);
  41    event→decision latency and decision wall median / p95; A's ticks; heat pauses, time to the first, total pause time,
  42    ACTIVE share; skin start / max (readings and heat trace) / end; minimum CPU caps at readings; camera cycle period
  43    (within one ACTIVE slot), detect ms 320 / 640; MemAvailable minimum; energy; agreement of served scripted choices
  44    with the B2 run of record (`b2_rules/runs/20261102T143251Z`, TEST V3 FWD, SHA-256 pinned).
  45  - **Frozen definition:** SHA-256 of `oneshot.sh`, the run of record, the TEST set, every imported module under the
  46    repo, and the server command; `--resume` refuses any difference.
  47  - **Run** (native Termux, no agent): `bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh`, then `… oneshot.sh
  48    --resume <UTC>` until `all arms done`. `--smoke`: each arm 180 s, gate before the first arm only, agent/cores
  49    checks recorded but not enforced, SMOKE - NOT A MEASUREMENT. Smoke only: the deployed HeatControl runs with
  50    lowered thresholds set per arm from that arm's start reading (after server load and camera start): pause at arm-
  51    start skin + 1.0 °C, resume at pause − 0.5 °C; its 60 s active / 30 s pause rules unchanged; set at runtime,
  52    `heat_control.py` unchanged. The smoke report gives each arm's thresholds and its pauses (camera stop) and resumes
  53    (camera start), and says when no pause or no resume happened. Timed runs use 37/35 °C. `oneshot.sh` is B4's
  54    launcher with only its paths, runner name and header changed (a shell script cannot import B4's).
  55  - Writes `~/robot_runs/mind_b5/<UTC>/`: run.json, sessionNN.json, sessionNN_end.json, arms.jsonl, requests.jsonl,
  56    NN_<arm>_server.log, heat_trace_<UTC>.jsonl, slots/, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
  57  - Offline tests: `python -B test_b5.py` (fakes and a fake clock; one short real-time arm with the deployed
  58    HeatControl over a fake monitor; imports B4's test fixtures).
  59  - Known limits: battery % is seen only at readings (no extra sampler); a person flickering between frames produces
  60    several real events (the queue of one absorbs them); B4's carried-over interrupt/cleanup limitation.
END

D4 BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh (143 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
   2  # MIND-B5: 30-min motors-off mission (RobotCam+YOLO, heat pause, V3 picker arms A, B); no motors, serial port or
   3  # ESP32. Start from NATIVE Termux (prompt "~ $"), no agent running, charger unplugged, battery >= 85 %, Termux in
   4  # front, screen on:
   5  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh                  (session 1: a new run)
   6  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh --resume <UTC>   (next session: remaining arms)
   7  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh --smoke          (180 s arms, NOT A MEASUREMENT;
   8  #                                                                               an agent may stay resident)
   9  # B4's oneshot.sh (itself MEM-1's) with this folder's runner: same root, battery, lock, screen-timeout and signal
  10  # handling, the same 15 s root thermal logger (pinned to cores 0-3 at nice 10; the skin gate reads its log).
  11  # Root commands that call Android services use the DECISIONS #123 form:
  12  # su -c "<cmd> </dev/null >/data/local/tmp/<file> 2>&1", then the file is read.
  13  H=/data/data/com.termux/files/home
  14  L=$H/robot_runs/mind_b5
  15  R=$H/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission
  16  S=/data/local/tmp/mind_b5_screen.txt
  17  mkdir -p "$L"
  18  CONSOLE=$L/oneshot_console_$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ).log
  19  say() { echo "[oneshot] $*" | tee -a "$CONSOLE"; }
  20  
  21  if [ -d /termux-home ] || [ "${PREFIX:-}" != /data/data/com.termux/files/usr ]; then
  22    echo "Run this from native Termux (~ \$), not from Debian."; exit 1
  23  fi
  24  su -c id >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "No root: su failed. Check Magisk."; exit 1; }
  25  [ -f "$R/b5.py" ] || { echo "Missing $R/b5.py"; exit 1; }
  26  case "$*" in
  27    ""|"--smoke"|"--resume "[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]T[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]Z) ;;
  28    *) echo "Usage: oneshot.sh [--smoke | --resume <UTC>]"; exit 1;;
  29  esac
  30  if pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex' >/dev/null; then
  31    if [ "$*" = --smoke ]; then
  32      echo "[oneshot] agent running (allowed for --smoke only; NOT A MEASUREMENT):"; pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex'
  33    else
  34      echo "An agent is still running:"; pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex'; echo "Quit it, then rerun."; exit 1
  35    fi
  36  fi
  37  if pgrep -fa 'llama-server|chat\.py' >/dev/null; then
  38    echo "llama-server or chat.py is already running:"; pgrep -fa 'llama-server|chat\.py'; echo "Stop it, then rerun."; exit 1
  39  fi
  40  RUNNERS='b4\.py|bc\.py|bv\.py|b[0-9]b?\.py|mem1\.py|chat_mtp\.py|repro\.py|vis3d\.py|power_map\.py|coresidency\.py|thermal_char\.py|vis[0-9]_run\.py|run_mission\.py|main\.py'
  41  if pgrep -fa "$RUNNERS" >/dev/null; then
  42    echo "A benchmark runner or the robot is already running:"; pgrep -fa "$RUNNERS"; echo "Stop it, then rerun."; exit 1
  43  fi
  44  check_battery() {  # checked at start and again right before the runner
  45    BAT=$(su -c 'cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status' 2>/dev/null)
  46    if [ "$BAT" != Discharging ]; then
  47      echo "[oneshot] Battery status is '$BAT', not Discharging: unplug the charger (power numbers would be invalid)." | tee -a "$CONSOLE"
  48      exit 1
  49    fi
  50  }
  51  cleanup() {
  52    [ -n "$LOCKED" ] || return 0  # refused before taking the lock: another run's files are not ours to touch
  53    touch "$L/.stop_thermal"
  54    if [ "$SCREEN_SET" = 1 ] && [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
  55      su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
  56      su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
  57      BACK=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
  58      if [ "$BACK" = "$OLD_TIMEOUT" ]; then
  59        say "screen timeout restored to $OLD_TIMEOUT ms; read back: $BACK"
  60      else
  61        say "WARNING: screen timeout NOT restored (read back '$BACK', expected $OLD_TIMEOUT). Run in native Termux:"
  62        say "  su -c \"settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1\""
  63        RESTORE_FAILED=1
  64      fi
  65    fi
  66    [ -z "${PAUSE:-}" ] || kill "$PAUSE" 2>/dev/null
  67    termux-wake-unlock
  68    # the lock goes only once the logger has exited (it polls .stop_thermal every 5 s):
  69    # otherwise a new run could remove .stop_thermal under it and share its files with it
  70    JOBS="${LOGGER:-}"
  71    for i in $(seq 40); do
  72      ALIVE=; for p in $JOBS; do kill -0 "$p" 2>/dev/null && ALIVE=1; done
  73      [ -z "$ALIVE" ] && break
  74      sleep 0.5
  75    done
  76    if [ -n "$ALIVE" ]; then
  77      say "WARNING: logger still running after 20 s; $L/.lock kept. Remove it when they have stopped."
  78      RESTORE_FAILED=1
  79    else
  80      say "stopped logger"
  81      rm -rf "$L/.lock"
  82    fi
  83    [ -z "${RESTORE_FAILED:-}" ] || exit 1
  84  }
  85  LOCKED=
  86  SCREEN_SET=0
  87  trap cleanup EXIT
  88  RUNNER=
  89  LAUNCHING=
  90  PENDING=
  91  on_signal() {  # before the runner starts: exit (the EXIT trap cleans up); after: forward it and keep waiting
  92    if [ -n "$RUNNER" ]; then kill -TERM "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
  93    elif [ -n "$LAUNCHING" ]; then PENDING=1  # between `&` and RUNNER=$!: forwarded right after
  94    else exit "$1"; fi
  95  }
  96  trap 'on_signal 143' TERM; trap 'on_signal 129' HUP; trap 'on_signal 130' INT
  97  # one run at a time: runs share thermal.log, .stop_thermal and port 8080
  98  if ! mkdir "$L/.lock" 2>/dev/null; then
  99    echo "Another MIND-B5 launcher holds $L/.lock (pid $(cat "$L/.lock/pid" 2>/dev/null))."
 100    echo "If no run is active (for example after a crash), remove it: rm -r $L/.lock"; exit 1
 101  fi
 102  LOCKED=1
 103  echo $$ > "$L/.lock/pid"
 104  check_battery
 105  
 106  termux-wake-lock
 107  pause() { sleep "$1" & PAUSE=$!; wait "$PAUSE"; PAUSE=; }  # a trapped signal interrupts wait, not a foreground sleep
 108  # keep the screen on (Android moves Termux off the big cores when it is not in front)
 109  OLD_TIMEOUT=
 110  if su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"; then
 111    OLD_TIMEOUT=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
 112  fi
 113  if ! [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
 114    say "Could not read the screen timeout (read '$OLD_TIMEOUT': $(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | head -c 200)); not started."; exit 1
 115  fi
 116  SCREEN_SET=1  # from here on, cleanup restores the validated old value
 117  su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout 2147483647 </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
 118  su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
 119  NOW=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
 120  if [ "$NOW" != 2147483647 ]; then
 121    say "Could not set the screen timeout (read back '$NOW'); not started."; exit 1
 122  fi
 123  say "screen timeout was $OLD_TIMEOUT ms; set to 2147483647 for the run (read back)"
 124  [ "$OLD_TIMEOUT" = 2147483647 ] && say "WARNING: the saved timeout is already 2147483647 (left by an earlier run?); set it yourself afterwards"
 125  rm -f "$L/.stop_thermal" "$L/thermal_logger_sched.txt"
 126  
 127  su -c "taskset -p f \$\$ >/dev/null 2>&1; renice -n 10 -p \$\$ >/dev/null 2>&1; { grep Cpus_allowed_list /proc/\$\$/status; echo nice=\$(cut -d' ' -f19 /proc/\$\$/stat); } > $L/thermal_logger_sched.txt 2>&1; while [ ! -f $L/.stop_thermal ]; do echo \"\$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) z9=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/temp) z10=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone10/temp) z11=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone11/temp) CPU-core_reading,_not_a_heat_state=1\" >> $L/thermal.log; sleep 15; done" &
 128  LOGGER=$!
 129  
 130  pause 6
 131  say "CPU-core reading, not a heat state: $(tail -1 "$L/thermal.log")"
 132  check_battery
 133  
 134  # The runner runs as a job so TERM/HUP/INT sent to this launcher reach it: it stops llama-server
 135  # itself, and we wait for that before the EXIT trap restores the screen timeout.
 136  LAUNCHING=1
 137  env PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python -u "$R/b5.py" "$@" > >(tee -a "$CONSOLE") 2>&1 &
 138  RUNNER=$!
 139  [ -z "$PENDING" ] || kill -TERM "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
 140  while kill -0 "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null; do wait "$RUNNER"; done
 141  wait "$RUNNER"; RC=$?
 142  if [ "$RC" = 0 ]; then say "done; console log: $CONSOLE"; else say "RUNNER FAILED (exit $RC); console log: $CONSOLE"; fi
 143  exit "$RC"
END

## E. Unchanged context (imported code; numbered; full files where marked)
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-B4: timed baselines measured alone (no agent resident): one picker decision, one vision call (70 and 140
   3  image tokens), RobotCam+YOLO, idle, and the cost of 5 s monitoring. Research only: no motors (motors.py is never
   4  imported), no serial port, no mission, no MTP. Timings count only from an owner run started by oneshot.sh in native
   5  Termux.
   6  
   7  Server (P, P0, PT, V70, V140, PY): BC's b11716 command, bc.command(<run>/slots) = mem1.command(2048) (setup_q4 flags)
   8  with the b11716 llama-server, --slot-save-path <run>/slots and VIS-3D's projector flags (Q8_0, --image-min/max-tokens
   9  70); V140 sets --image-min-tokens and --image-max-tokens to 140 (server_command; the only difference). Pins bc.PINS.
  10  One server per block, started after the gate and stopped at the block end; load and warm-up are not timed. Request
  11  timeout REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S (60 s).
  12  Picker: B2 V3 unchanged (b2.preflight / b2.score: rules, examples, reason sentence, two-step scoring), no memory, on
  13  B2 TEST items 1-20 (b2_rules/set/b2_test.json, SHA-256 pinned) in FWD order, repeated. Before the block: the 20
  14  prompts are rendered and checked (b2.preflight), the common prefix of their ids is sent once (n_predict 1,
  15  cache_prompt true) and slot 0 is saved. Before every 5th decision (5th, 10th, ...) slot 0 is restored.
  16  Vision (V70, V140): BV ids FRAME_IDS (frame SHA-256 checked against results.json), bv.body: BV's prompt and settings,
  17  one /v1/chat/completions call each, in that order, repeated; same frames, prompt and settings in both blocks.
  18  Camera: main.Robot(motors=None) is created first, then start_camera() (CAMERA_RATE 1, PinnedDetector on cpus 4-5,
  19  CAM1-MID #136); stop_camera() is attempted whenever a start was attempted, also when reading the detector's
  20  affinity at the stop fails (that failure makes the block INVALID). Each cycle = run_cycle's vision step (a new
  21  frame of this session, SizePolicy size 640 every 5 s else 320, detect, observe) followed by the dry run's move time
  22  (main.CYCLE_MOVE_TIME; the last one is cut at the deadline); no navigation rules, picker, M2 or heat control. A frame
  23  that is not new ('no new') is re-read after 0.1 s, at most NO_NEW_MAX times per block; any other camera stop (an
  24  unusable frame) ends the phase and makes the block INVALID. After stop_camera, capture must show stopped (frame files
  25  gone) within CAMERA_STOP_S.
  26  
  27  Time ceiling: each work phase has an absolute deadline (phase start + BLOCK_S); no decision (with its restore), call
  28  or camera cycle starts after it, the one in flight may finish; the overrun is recorded and the block is VALID only
  29  if it is <= OVERRUN_MAX_S (15 s). A slot restore that ends at or after the deadline is recorded as a restore-only
  30  row and ends the phase; its decision never starts. P0 (two fixed cold decisions, seconds only) has no deadline.
  31  Blocks, in ORDER: I idle (screen on), P picker, P0 two cold decisions (item 1 after /slots erase = full prefix
  32  rebuild, then item 1 after slot restore; not a heat block), PT = P plus the root sampler (every SAMPLE_S s: skin,
  33  status, CPU caps, battery; its thread must get cores 0-3 at nice 10, else PT INVALID), V70 and V140 vision, Y
  34  camera+YOLO without llama-server, PY = a P phase, then right away (no gate) a Y phase with the server loaded and
  35  idle (the hand-off reading is taken after the camera start, so the start's cost falls in the P phase).
  36  Before every block: cores 4-7 (cr.wait_cores, then the check), agent check (repro.agents), MEM-1's skin gate
  37  (pm.thermal_gate: VIRTUAL-SKIN <= launch-start skin + 1.0 degC, waiting idle at most GATE_MAX_S = 30 min per
  38  block, set into pm.GATE_MAX_S; the wait is recorded as cooling data; --smoke keeps pm's default and gates only the
  39  first block), Android status < 3 and battery < 45 degC. A gate not passed, cores not available or an agent found ends
  40  the session before the block. run_block checks agents and cores again before any workload setup and aborts (INVALID)
  41  when they fail. During every block a watcher thread (cores 0-3, nice 10, same in all blocks) scans /proc every
  42  WATCH_S s, at the work start and once more at its end: agent processes (claude|agy|codex|node in a cmdline), the
  43  runner's cores 4-7, every llama-server thread's affinity 4-7 (/proc/<pid>/task; a thread that exists but cannot
  44  be read is a hit; one that disappeared before its read is skipped and counted as 'vanished' in the scan record)
  45  and the detector threads' 4-5; any hit makes the block INVALID. The
  46  resolution is WATCH_S (30 s): an agent that lives entirely between two scans is not seen.
  47  Start and end of each block (PY also at the hand-off): reading(): VIRTUAL-SKIN, Android thermal status, cpufreq caps
  48  policy0/4/6, battery %, temp, charge counter and voltage, Pss/Rss/VmHWM/VmRSS of llama-server, the runner (detector)
  49  and RobotCam, MemAvailable; every section's return code and every required field is checked (reading_problems),
  50  Pss and VmHWM per process before they are summed.
  51  Labels: block VALID only with every check clean (block_problems); an INVALID block ends the session and is redone in
  52  a later one. Server exit code != 0 after SIGTERM is a FINDING (#145), not a validity input. P vs PT COMPARABLE only
  53  when both are VALID with start skin within 1.0 degC and battery within 10 % (comparable()); other cross-block numbers
  54  are descriptive. Heat rate = (end skin - start skin) / minutes. Energy from the charge counter is INDICATIVE.
  55  Frozen definition (definition()): at the first session, SHA-256 of oneshot.sh, b4.py and every imported module file
  56  under the repo, the full server command of every server block and the SHA-256 of the vision frame files; --resume
  57  refuses any difference.
  58  
  59  Usage: b4.py                 a new run, first session (native Termux, via oneshot.sh)
  60         b4.py --resume RUN    the next session of RUN (its folder name in ~/robot_runs/mind_b4)
  61         b4.py --smoke         every block once for SMOKE_S s, gate before the first block only (pm's default
  62                               wait), agent/cores checks recorded but not enforced; SMOKE - NOT A MEASUREMENT
  63  Writes ~/robot_runs/mind_b4/<UTC>[_smoke]/: run.json, sessionNN.json, sessionNN_end.json, blocks.jsonl,
  64  requests.jsonl, NN_<block>_server.log, slots/, report.txt, artifacts.sha256 (the last two rewritten per session).

 116  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- readings
 117  
 118  def caps():
 119      out = {}
 120      for p in POLICIES:
 121          try:
 122              out[p] = int(Path(f'/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpufreq/{p}/scaling_max_freq').read_text())
 123          except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
 124              out[p] = f'error: {e}'
 125      return out
 126  
 127  
 128  def root_script(pids, light):
 129      """One su call (DECISIONS #123 form via cr.root): its shell asks for cores 0-3 and nice 10; battery, dumpsys
 130      thermalservice and, unless light, memory of each pid and of every RobotCam process."""
 131      b = mem1.BATTERY
 132      s = ('taskset -p f $$ >/dev/null 2>&1; renice -n 10 -p $$ >/dev/null 2>&1; '
 133           f'echo "=== battery"; cat {b}/capacity {b}/status {b}/temp {b}/charge_counter {b}/voltage_now; echo "rc=$?"; '
 134           'echo "=== thermalservice"; dumpsys thermalservice; echo "rc=$?"; ')
 135      if light:
 136          return s.rstrip('; ')  # cr.root wraps it in '{ ...; }': a trailing ';' would make '; ;', a syntax error
 137      mem = 'grep -hE "^(Pss|Rss):" /proc/{0}/smaps_rollup || r=1; grep -hE "^Vm(HWM|RSS):" /proc/{0}/status || r=1; '
 138      for name, pid in pids.items():
 139          s += f'echo "=== mem_{name}"; r=0; echo "pid: {int(pid)}"; {mem.format(int(pid))}echo "rc=$r"; '
 140      return s + (f'echo "=== mem_robotcam"; r=0; for p in $(pidof {ROBOTCAM}); do echo "pid: $p"; {mem.format("$p")}'
 141                  'done; echo "rc=$r"')
 142  
 143  
 144  def mem_section(text):
 145      """kB per pid ('per_pid'), then summed over the section's processes; 'missing' names every pid without Pss or
 146      VmHWM (one process's field never stands in for another's)."""
 147      m, per = {'pids': []}, {}
 148      for line in text.splitlines():
 149          k, _, v = line.partition(':')
 150          if k == 'pid':
 151              m['pids'].append(int(v))
 152              per[int(v)] = {}
 153          elif k in ('Pss', 'Rss', 'VmHWM', 'VmRSS') and v.split() and m['pids']:
 154              per[m['pids'][-1]][k] = int(v.split()[0])
 155      for f in per.values():
 156          for k, v in f.items():
 157              m[k] = m.get(k, 0) + v
 158      m['per_pid'] = per
 159      m['missing'] = [f'pid {p} {k}' for p, f in per.items() for k in ('Pss', 'VmHWM') if k not in f]
 160      return m
 161  
 162  
 163  def reading(pids=None, light=False, tag='b4_read'):
 164      """Skin, status, caps, battery (+ charge counter, voltage); unless light, MemAvailable and process memory. Every
 165      section's return code is kept (None: the section did not finish)."""
 166      c = {'utc': cr.utc(), 'mono': round(time.monotonic(), 3), 'caps': caps()}
 167      if not light:
 168          c['avail_kb'] = next(int(l.split()[1]) for l in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines()
 169                               if l.startswith('MemAvailable'))
 170      began = time.monotonic()
 171      try:
 172          rc, out = cr.root(root_script(pids or {}, light), tag, timeout=ROOT_TIMEOUT_S)
 173      except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
 174          rc, out = None, ''
 175          c['root_error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'[:300]
 176      c.update(root_rc=rc, root_s=round(time.monotonic() - began, 3))
 177      if rc != 0:
 178          c['root_stderr'] = cr.root_stderr()[:300]
 179      sec = mem1.sections(out)
 180      c['battery_rc'], text = sec.get('battery', (None, ''))
 181      try:
 182          pct, status, temp, charge, volt = text.split()[:5]
 183          c.update(battery_pct=int(pct), battery_status=status, battery_c=int(temp) / 10, charge_uah=int(charge),
 184                   voltage_uv=int(volt))
 185      except ValueError:
 186          c['battery_error'] = text[:300]
 187      c['dump_rc'], text = sec.get('thermalservice', (None, ''))
 188      d = cr.parse_dump(text)
 189      c.update(status=d['status'], skin=d['skin'])
 190      for name, (rc_m, text) in sec.items():
 191          if name.startswith('mem_'):
 192              c[name] = {**mem_section(text), 'rc': rc_m}
 193      return c
 194  
 195  
 196  REQUIRED = ('skin', 'status', 'battery_pct', 'charge_uah', 'voltage_uv')
 197  
 198  
 199  def reading_problems(c, label, need=()):
 200      """Every section's return code and every required field: skin, status, caps, battery %, charge counter, voltage;
 201      Pss and VmHWM of every pid of each section in need."""
 202      why = []
 203      if c.get('root_rc') != 0:
 204          why.append(f'{label}: root rc {c.get("root_rc")} {c.get("root_error", "")}'.strip())
 205      why += [f'{label}: {k} {c.get(k)}' for k in ('battery_rc', 'dump_rc') if c.get(k) != 0]
 206      why += [f'{label}: {k} missing' for k in REQUIRED if c.get(k) is None]
 207      why += [f'{label}: {p} cap {v}' for p, v in c.get('caps', {}).items() if not isinstance(v, int)]
 208      for n in need:
 209          m = c.get(f'mem_{n}', {})
 210          why += [f'{label}: {n} section rc {m.get("rc")}'] if m.get('rc') != 0 else []
 211          why += [f'{label}: {n} {k} missing' for k in ('Pss', 'VmHWM') if not m.get(k)]
 212          why += [f'{label}: {n} {x} missing' for x in m.get('missing', [])]
 213      return why
 214  
 215  
 216  class Periodic:
 217      """fn() at once, then every period s, and once more when stopped, from a thread asking for cores 0-3 at nice 10
 218      (mem1.pin_sampler; what it got is recorded). A slow call skips the missed slots."""
 219  
 220      def __init__(self, fn, period):
 221          self.fn, self.period, self.samples, self.sched, self.error = fn, period, [], None, None
 222          self.halt = threading.Event()
 223          self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.loop, daemon=True)
 224  
 225      def start(self):
 226          self.thread.start()
 227          return self
 228  
 229      def loop(self):
 230          try:
 231              self.sched = mem1.pin_sampler()
 232              t0, last = time.monotonic(), False
 233              while True:
 234                  self.samples.append(self.fn())
 235                  if last:
 236                      return
 237                  k = int((time.monotonic() - t0) / self.period) + 1
 238                  last = self.halt.wait(max(0.0, t0 + k * self.period - time.monotonic()))
 239          except Exception as e:
 240              self.error = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 241  
 242      def stop(self):
 243          self.halt.set()
 244          self.thread.join(ROOT_TIMEOUT_S + 5)
 245          return {'period_s': self.period, 'sched': self.sched, 'error': self.error, 'alive': self.thread.is_alive(),
 246                  'samples': self.samples}
 247  
 248  
 249  def Sampler(read=None, period=SAMPLE_S):
 250      """PT's root sampler: reading(light) every period s."""
 251      return Periodic(read or (lambda: reading(light=True, tag='b4_sample')), period)
 252  
 253  
 254  def thread_problems(s, name):
 255      why = [f'{name}: {s["error"]}'] if s['error'] else []
 256      why += [f'{name}: still running after its join'] if s['alive'] else []
 257      got = s.get('sched') or {}
 258      if got.get('cpus') != THREAD_CPUS or got.get('nice') != THREAD_NICE:
 259          why.append(f'{name}: got cpus {got.get("cpus")} nice {got.get("nice")}, need {THREAD_CPUS} nice {THREAD_NICE}')
 260      return why
 261  
 262  
 263  def sampler_problems(s, start_mono, end_mono):
 264      why = thread_problems(s, 'sampler')
 265      ok = [x['mono'] for x in s['samples'] if not reading_problems(x, 'sample')]
 266      if len(ok) < len(s['samples']):
 267          why.append(f'sampler: {len(s["samples"]) - len(ok)} of {len(s["samples"])} samples failed')
 268      marks = [start_mono] + [m for m in ok if start_mono <= m <= end_mono] + [end_mono]
 269      gap = max(b - a for a, b in zip(marks, marks[1:]))
 270      if gap > SAMPLE_MAX_GAP_S:
 271          why.append(f'sampler: gap {gap:.1f} s > {SAMPLE_MAX_GAP_S} s')
 272      return why
 273  
 274  
 275  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- agent and cores
 276  
 277  def cores_ok():
 278      return set(range(4, 8)) <= cr.allowed_cpus()
 279  
 280  
 281  def parse_cpus(text):
 282      cpus = set()
 283      for part in text.strip().split(','):
 284          a, _, b = part.partition('-')
 285          cpus.update(range(int(a), int(b or a) + 1))
 286      return cpus
 287  
 288  
 289  PROC = Path('/proc')  # llama-server's threads are read here (tests point it at a fake tree)
 290  
 291  
 292  def watch_check(targets):
 293      """One light /proc scan: agent processes, the runner's cores 4-7, the affinity of every llama-server thread
 294      (targets['server_pid'], PROC/<pid>/task/*) 4-7, the detector threads' (targets['detector']) 4-5; an unreadable
 295      thread list, or a thread that exists but cannot be read, is a hit; a thread that disappeared between the listing
 296      and its read (FileNotFoundError / ProcessLookupError) is skipped and counted in 'vanished', not a hit.
 297      Returns {'mono', 'hits', 'vanished'} (no hits: clean)."""
 298      hits, vanished = [], 0
 299      for p in Path('/proc').iterdir():
 300          if p.name.isdigit():
 301              try:
 302                  cmd = (p / 'cmdline').read_bytes().replace(b'\0', b' ').decode(errors='replace')
 303              except OSError:
 304                  continue
 305              if AGENT_RE.search(cmd):
 306                  hits.append(f'agent pid {p.name}: {cmd[:100]}')
 307      if not cores_ok():
 308          hits.append(f'runner cores {sorted(cr.allowed_cpus())}')
 309      if (pid := targets.get('server_pid')) is not None:
 310          try:
 311              tids = sorted(t.name for t in (PROC / str(pid) / 'task').iterdir())
 312          except OSError as e:
 313              tids = []
 314              hits.append(f'llama-server threads unreadable: {e}')
 315          for tid in tids:  # affinity is per thread: every one, not only the leader
 316              try:
 317                  line = next(l for l in (PROC / str(pid) / 'task' / tid / 'status').read_text().splitlines()
 318                              if l.startswith('Cpus_allowed_list'))
 319                  if parse_cpus(line.split(':', 1)[1]) != SERVER_CPUS:
 320                      hits.append(f'llama-server thread {tid} {line}')
 321              except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
 322                  vanished += 1  # the thread ended after the listing
 323              except (OSError, StopIteration, ValueError) as e:
 324                  hits.append(f'llama-server thread {tid} affinity unreadable: {e}')
 325      if (det := targets.get('detector')) is not None:
 326          bad = {t: c for t, c in det.affinity().items() if c != DETECTOR_CPUS}
 327          if bad:
 328              hits.append(f'detector threads {bad}')
 329      return {'mono': round(time.monotonic(), 3), 'hits': hits, 'vanished': vanished}
 330  
 331  
 332  def Watch(targets, period=WATCH_S):
 333      return Periodic(lambda: watch_check(targets), period)
 334  
 335  
 336  def watch_problems(w):
 337      why = thread_problems(w, 'watch')
 338      why += [f'watch: {h}' for x in w['samples'] for h in x['hits']]
 339      return why if w['samples'] else why + ['watch: no scan']
 340  
 341  
 342  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- checks before a block
 343  
 344  def gate(ref_skin, label):
 345      """MEM-1's skin gate (pm.thermal_gate with cr.SKIN_GATE_C = 1.0)."""
 346      g = pm.thermal_gate(THERMAL_LOG, {'skin': ref_skin}, label, False)
 347      return {**g, 'passed': not g['warm_start']}
 348  
 349  
 350  def comparable(p, pt):
 351      """P vs PT: (COMPARABLE | NOT COMPARABLE, reasons)."""
 352      why = [f'{r["block"]} {r["label"]}' for r in (p, pt) if r['label'] != 'VALID']
 353      if not why:
 354          a, b = p['start'], pt['start']
 355          if abs(a['skin'] - b['skin']) > SKIN_MATCH_C:
 356              why.append(f'start skin {a["skin"]} vs {b["skin"]} degC differ by more than {SKIN_MATCH_C}')
 357          if abs(a['battery_pct'] - b['battery_pct']) > BATTERY_MATCH_PCT:
 358              why.append(f'battery {a["battery_pct"]} vs {b["battery_pct"]} % differ by more than {BATTERY_MATCH_PCT}')
 359      return ('NOT COMPARABLE' if why else 'COMPARABLE'), why
 360  
 361  
 362  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- server and picker
 363  
 364  def post(path, body):
 365      return cr.post(path, body, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S)
 366  
 367  
 368  def server_command(out, block):
 369      """bc.command(<run>/slots); V140 sets --image-min-tokens and --image-max-tokens to 140."""
 370      cmd = bc.command(out / 'slots')
 371      for flag in ('--image-min-tokens', '--image-max-tokens'):
 372          cmd[cmd.index(flag) + 1] = str(VISION_BLOCKS.get(block, vis3d.IMAGE_TOKENS))
 373      return cmd
 374  
 375  
 376  def start_server(out, name, cmd):
 377      """(process, log file, load seconds), started by this process only."""
 378      log = open(out / f'{name}_server.log', 'x')
 379      t0 = time.monotonic()
 380      proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
 381      while not cr.healthy():
 382          if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > b0.LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 383              stop_server(proc, log)
 384              raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 385          time.sleep(0.1)
 386      return proc, log, round(time.monotonic() - t0, 3)
 387  
 388  
 389  def stop_server(proc, log):
 390      """(exit code, SIGKILL needed)."""
 391      killed = False
 392      if proc.poll() is None:
 393          os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 394          try:
 395              proc.wait(timeout=b0.TERM_WAIT_S)
 396          except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 397              os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 398              proc.wait()
 399              killed = True
 400      log.close()
 401      return proc.returncode, killed
 402  
 403  
 404  def slot(action):
 405      t0 = time.perf_counter()
 406      res = post(f'/slots/0?action={action}', {'filename': b0b.SLOT_FILE})
 407      return {'action': action, 'wall_s': round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3), 'response': res}
 408  
 409  
 410  def picker_setup(test):
 411      """(set of items 1-20, prompts, system prefix ids): b2.preflight's V3 FWD prompts; any problem raises."""
 412      s = {**test, 'items': [it for it in test['items'] if it['id'] in ITEMS]}
 413      info = {'problems': []}
 414      prompts = b2.preflight(post, s, [('V3', 'FWD')], info, 'TEST')
 415      if info['problems'] or len(s['items']) != len(ITEMS):
 416          raise RuntimeError(f'picker preflight: {info["problems"]} ({len(s["items"])} items)')
 417      ids = [prompts['V3', 'FWD', it['id']][1] for it in s['items']]
 418      return s, prompts, ids[0][:min(mem1.lcp(ids[0], x) for x in ids[1:])]
 419  
 420  
 421  def warm(prefix):
 422      """The system prefix once (not timed), then slot 0 saved."""
 423      res = post('/completion', {'prompt': prefix, **b0.DECODE, 'n_predict': 1, 'cache_prompt': True})
 424      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 425      return {'prefix_tokens': len(prefix), 'prompt_n': tm.get('prompt_n'), 'cache_n': tm.get('cache_n'),
 426              'save': slot('save')}
 427  
 428  
 429  class Recorder:
 430      """post() for one decision: keeps every /completion response (V3 sends two) and the time spent per path."""
 431  
 432      def __init__(self):
 433          self.completions, self.seconds = [], {}
 434  
 435      def __call__(self, path, body):
 436          t0 = time.perf_counter()
 437          res = post(path, body)
 438          self.seconds[path] = round(self.seconds.get(path, 0) + time.perf_counter() - t0, 3)
 439          if path == '/completion':
 440              self.completions.append(res)
 441          return res
 442  
 443  
 444  def decide(s, it, prompt):
 445      """One B2 V3 decision (b2.score unchanged): wall, reason tokens, step-1 generation tok/s, both steps' prompt_n and
 446      cache_n; seconds per endpoint (b2's /tokenize checks are harness overhead, reported separately)."""
 447      rec, row = Recorder(), {'id': it['id']}
 448      t0 = time.perf_counter()
 449      try:
 450          r = b2.score(rec, it, 'V3', 'FWD', prompt)
 451          t1, t2 = (x.get('timings', {}) for x in rec.completions)
 452          row.update(top=r['top'], reason=r['reason'], reason_tokens=r['reason_tokens'],
 453                     gen_tok_s=t1.get('predicted_per_second'), step1_prompt_n=t1.get('prompt_n'),
 454                     step1_cache_n=t1.get('cache_n'), step1_prompt_ms=t1.get('prompt_ms'),
 455                     step2_prompt_n=t2.get('prompt_n'), step2_cache_n=t2.get('cache_n'), step2_prompt_ms=t2.get('prompt_ms'))
 456      except Exception as e:
 457          row['error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 458      row.update(wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3), endpoint_s=rec.seconds)
 459      return row
 460  
 461  
 462  def run_picker(ps, deadline, emit):
 463      """Decisions on items 1-20 in order, repeated; none (with its restore) starts at or after the deadline; restore
 464      before every 5th. A restore that ends at or after the deadline is emitted as a restore-only row (kind 'restore',
 465      not a decision) and ends the phase: its decision never starts."""
 466      s, prompts = ps[0], ps[1]
 467      t0, n = clock(), 0
 468      while clock() < deadline:
 469          it = s['items'][n % len(s['items'])]
 470          row = {'kind': 'decision', 'n': n + 1}
 471          if (n + 1) % RESTORE_EVERY == 0:
 472              try:
 473                  row['restore'] = slot('restore')
 474              except Exception as e:
 475                  row['error'] = f'restore: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 476              if 'error' not in row and clock() >= deadline:
 477                  row['kind'] = 'restore'
 478          if 'error' not in row and row['kind'] == 'decision':
 479              row.update(decide(s, it, prompts['V3', 'FWD', it['id']]))
 480          row.update(t=round(clock() - t0, 3), cores_ok=cores_ok())
 481          emit(row)
 482          if 'error' in row or row['kind'] == 'restore':
 483              return
 484          n += 1
 485  
 486  
 487  def run_p0(ps, emit):
 488      """Item 1 after /slots erase (full prefix rebuild), then item 1 after slot restore; no deadline."""
 489      s, prompts = ps[0], ps[1]
 490      it = s['items'][0]
 491      for kind, action in (('cold_erase', 'erase'), ('cold_restore', 'restore')):
 492          row = {'kind': kind}
 493          try:
 494              row[action] = slot(action)
 495              row.update(decide(s, it, prompts['V3', 'FWD', it['id']]))
 496          except Exception as e:
 497              row['error'] = f'{action}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 498          row['cores_ok'] = cores_ok()
 499          emit(row)
 500          if 'error' in row:
 501              return
 502  
 503  
 504  def frames():
 505      """{id: (results.json record, base64 JPEG)} for FRAME_IDS; a frame whose SHA-256 differs raises."""
 506      names = sorted(p.name for p in bv.FRAMES_DIR.glob('*.jpg'))
 507      ph = bv.photos(json.loads(bv.RESULTS.read_bytes()), names)
 508      out = {}
 509      for i in FRAME_IDS:
 510          data = (bv.FRAMES_DIR / Path(ph[i]['frame_file']).name).read_bytes()
 511          if sha(data) != ph[i]['frame_sha256']:
 512              raise SystemExit(f'refused: frame id {i} SHA-256 != results.json')
 513          out[i] = (ph[i], base64.b64encode(data).decode())
 514      return out
 515  
 516  
 517  def run_vision(fr, block, deadline, emit):
 518      t0, n = clock(), 0
 519      while clock() < deadline:
 520          i = FRAME_IDS[n % len(FRAME_IDS)]
 521          row = {'kind': 'vision', 'n': n + 1, 'id': i}
 522          c0 = time.perf_counter()
 523          try:
 524              res = post('/v1/chat/completions', bv.body(block, *fr[i]))
 525              tm = res.get('timings', {})
 526              row.update(content=res['choices'][0]['message'].get('content'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'),
 527                         prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'), cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'), predicted_n=tm.get('predicted_n'),
 528                         gen_tok_s=tm.get('predicted_per_second'))
 529          except Exception as e:
 530              row['error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 531          row.update(wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - c0, 3), t=round(clock() - t0, 3), cores_ok=cores_ok())
 532          emit(row)
 533          if 'error' in row:
 534              return
 535          n += 1
 536

 538  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- camera
 539  
 540  def make_robot():
 541      import main  # noqa: E402  imported here: never motors.py (main imports it nowhere; run_mission only when live)
 542      return main.Robot(None)
 543  
 544  
 545  def read_frame(**kw):
 546      import main
 547      return main.read_frame(**kw)
 548  
 549  
 550  def move_s():
 551      import main
 552      return main.CYCLE_MOVE_TIME
 553  
 554  
 555  def start_camera(robot, rec):
 556      """robot.start_camera() (main.Robot): pinned detector (created once per process), RobotCam at CAMERA_RATE."""
 557      import main
 558      t0 = time.monotonic()
 559      robot.start_camera()
 560      rec['camera'] = {'start_s': round(time.monotonic() - t0, 3), 'rate': main.CAMERA_RATE,
 561                       'detector': robot.detector.config(), 'affinity_start': robot.detector.affinity()}
 562  
 563  
 564  def stop_camera(robot):
 565      """main.Robot.stop_camera(), then capture must show stopped (frame files gone) within CAMERA_STOP_S."""
 566      t0 = time.monotonic()
 567      out = {}
 568      try:  # metadata in its own step: a failure here never skips the stop below
 569          out['affinity_end'] = robot.detector.affinity() if robot.detector else None
 570      except Exception as e:
 571          out['affinity_error'] = f'detector affinity at stop: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 572      try:
 573          robot.stop_camera()
 574      except Exception as e:
 575          return {**out, 'error': f'stop_camera: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'}
 576      while time.monotonic() - t0 < CAMERA_STOP_S:
 577          status = read_frame()['status']
 578          if status == 'missing':
 579              return {**out, 'stopped_s': round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2)}
 580          time.sleep(0.2)
 581      return {**out, 'error': f'capture not shown stopped {CAMERA_STOP_S} s after stop_camera (status {status})'}
 582  
 583  
 584  def run_camera(robot, deadline, emit):
 585      """run_cycle's vision step and the dry run's move time (cut at the deadline); no cycle starts at or after the
 586      deadline. 'no new' is re-read after 0.1 s (at most NO_NEW_MAX + 1 rows); any other stop ends the phase."""
 587      t0, last, no_new = clock(), None, 0
 588      while clock() < deadline:
 589          c0 = clock()
 590          r = read_frame(session=robot.camera_session)
 591          if r['status'] != 'ok' or (last is not None and r['frame'] <= last):
 592              reason = r['status'] if r['status'] != 'ok' else 'no new'
 593              emit({'kind': 'camera_stop', 'reason': reason, 't': round(c0 - t0, 3)})
 594              no_new += reason == 'no new'
 595              if reason != 'no new' or no_new > NO_NEW_MAX:
 596                  return
 597              pause(0.1)
 598              continue
 599          last, robot.camera_session = r['frame'], r['session']
 600          size = robot.size_policy.next_size()
 601          row = {'kind': 'cycle', 't': round(c0 - t0, 3), 'frame': r['frame'], 'age_s': r['age_s'], 'size': size}
 602          try:
 603              d0 = time.perf_counter()
 604              dets = robot.detector.detect(r['image'], size)
 605              row.update(detect_ms=round((time.perf_counter() - d0) * 1000, 1), n_det=len(dets))
 606              robot.size_policy.observe(dets, r['image'].height)
 607          except Exception as e:
 608              row['error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 609          row['cores_ok'] = cores_ok()
 610          emit(row)
 611          if 'error' in row:
 612              return
 613          pause(min(move_s(), max(0.0, deadline - clock())))
 614  
 615  
 616  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- one block
 617  
 618  def block_problems(rec, rows):
 619      """Why a block is INVALID (empty: VALID)."""
 620      need = {'start': ['runner'], 'end': ['runner'], 'handoff': ['runner']}
 621      if rec['block'] in SERVER_BLOCKS:
 622          for k in need:
 623              need[k].append('llama_server')
 624      if rec['block'] in ('Y', 'PY'):
 625          need['end'].append('robotcam')
 626          need['handoff' if rec['block'] == 'PY' else 'start'].append('robotcam')
 627      why = list(rec['errors'])
 628      for k in ('start', 'handoff', 'end'):
 629          if k in rec:
 630              why += reading_problems(rec[k], k, need[k])
 631          elif k != 'handoff' or rec['block'] == 'PY':
 632              why.append(f'{k} reading missing')
 633      if rec.get('agents_before') or rec.get('agents_after'):
 634          why.append(f'agent process running: {(rec.get("agents_before") or rec.get("agents_after"))[:120]!r}')
 635      if not rec.get('cores_before') or not rec.get('cores_after') or any(r.get('cores_ok') is False for r in rows):
 636          why.append('cores 4-7 not all allowed during the block')
 637      if n := sum('error' in r for r in rows):
 638          why.append(f'{n} request/cycle errors')
 639      if rec['block'] in ('P', 'PT', 'PY') and not any(r['kind'] == 'decision' for r in rows):
 640          why.append('no decisions')
 641      if rec['block'] == 'P0' and [r['kind'] for r in rows] != ['cold_erase', 'cold_restore']:
 642          why.append('P0 needs both cold decisions')
 643      if rec['block'] in VISION_BLOCKS and not rows:
 644          why.append('no vision calls')
 645      if rec['block'] in ('Y', 'PY') and not any(r['kind'] == 'cycle' for r in rows):
 646          why.append('no camera cycles')
 647      stops = [r['reason'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'camera_stop']
 648      why += [f'camera stop: {s}' for s in stops if s != 'no new']
 649      if stops.count('no new') > NO_NEW_MAX:
 650          why.append(f'{stops.count("no new")} "no new" frames > {NO_NEW_MAX}')
 651      why += [f'{p["phase"]} overrun {p["overrun_s"]} s > {OVERRUN_MAX_S} s' for p in rec.get('phases', [])
 652              if p['overrun_s'] > OVERRUN_MAX_S]
 653      why += [rec['camera_stop'][k] for k in ('affinity_error', 'error') if rec.get('camera_stop', {}).get(k)]
 654      if 'sampler' in rec:
 655          why += sampler_problems(rec['sampler'], rec.get('start', {}).get('mono', 0), rec.get('end', {}).get('mono', 0))
 656      if 'watch' in rec:
 657          why += watch_problems(rec['watch'])
 658      return why
 659  
 660  
 661  def phase(rec, name, work):
 662      """Runs work(deadline) with an absolute deadline BLOCK_S (or the smoke's) after now; records the overrun."""
 663      deadline = clock() + rec['work_s']
 664      work(deadline)
 665      end = clock()
 666      rec.setdefault('phases', []).append({'phase': name, 'overrun_s': round(max(0.0, end - deadline), 3)})
 667  
 668  
 669  def cleanup(rec, name, fn):
 670      """One cleanup action; its failure is recorded (block INVALID) and never skips the others."""
 671      try:
 672          fn()
 673      except BaseException as e:
 674          rec['errors'].append(f'cleanup {name}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
 675  
 676  
 677  def run_block(name, out, ctx, emit):
 678      """One block; its rows go to requests.jsonl through emit, its record is returned."""
 679      rec = {'block': name, 'session': ctx['session'], 'utc_start': cr.utc(), 'work_s': ctx['dur'], 'errors': [],
 680             'cores_before': cores_ok(), 'agents_before': repro.agents()}
 681      rows = []
 682  
 683      def add(row):
 684          row = {'block': name, 'session': ctx['session'], **row}
 685          rows.append(row)
 686          emit(row)
 687      if not ctx['smoke'] and (rec['agents_before'] or not rec['cores_before']):
 688          rec['errors'].append('initial agent/cores check failed: no workload started')
 689          rec.update(utc_end=cr.utc(), cores_after=cores_ok(), agents_after=repro.agents())
 690          rec['problems'] = block_problems(rec, rows)
 691          rec['label'] = 'INVALID'
 692          return rec, rows
 693      proc = log = robot = sampler = watch = None
 694      pids, targets = {'runner': os.getpid()}, {}
 695  
 696      def camera_on():
 697          nonlocal robot
 698          robot = make_robot()  # kept before the start: a start that fails half-way is still stopped
 699          start_camera(robot, rec)
 700          targets['detector'] = robot.detector
 701      try:
 702          if name in SERVER_BLOCKS:
 703              cmd = server_command(out, name)
 704              proc, log, rec['load_s'] = start_server(out, f'{ctx["session"]:02d}_{name}', cmd)
 705              pids['llama_server'] = targets['server_pid'] = proc.pid
 706              if name not in VISION_BLOCKS:
 707                  ps = picker_setup(ctx['test'])
 708                  rec['warm'] = warm(ps[2])
 709          if name == 'Y':
 710              camera_on()
 711          rec['start'] = reading(pids)
 712          watch = Watch(targets).start()
 713          if name == 'I':
 714              phase(rec, 'I', lambda d: pause(max(0.0, d - clock())))
 715          elif name in ('P', 'PT'):
 716              sampler = Sampler().start() if name == 'PT' else None
 717              phase(rec, name, lambda d: run_picker(ps, d, add))
 718          elif name == 'P0':
 719              run_p0(ps, add)
 720          elif name in VISION_BLOCKS:
 721              phase(rec, name, lambda d: run_vision(ctx['frames'], name, d, add))
 722          elif name == 'Y':
 723              phase(rec, 'Y', lambda d: run_camera(robot, d, add))
 724          elif name == 'PY':
 725              phase(rec, 'P phase', lambda d: run_picker(ps, d, add))
 726              if not any('error' in r for r in rows):
 727                  camera_on()
 728                  rec['handoff'] = reading(pids)
 729                  phase(rec, 'Y phase', lambda d: run_camera(robot, d, add))
 730          rec['end'] = reading(pids)
 731      except BaseException as e:  # recorded; cleanup below; a stop (signal) re-raises after the record is kept
 732          rec['errors'].append(f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}')
 733          if not isinstance(e, Exception):
 734              rec['interrupted'] = True
 735      finally:
 736          if watch:
 737              cleanup(rec, 'watch', lambda: rec.__setitem__('watch', watch.stop()))
 738          if sampler:
 739              cleanup(rec, 'sampler', lambda: rec.__setitem__('sampler', sampler.stop()))
 740          if robot:
 741              cleanup(rec, 'camera', lambda: rec.__setitem__('camera_stop', stop_camera(robot)))
 742          if proc:
 743              cleanup(rec, 'server', lambda: rec.update(zip(('exit_code', 'killed'), stop_server(proc, log))))
 744          rec.update(utc_end=cr.utc(), cores_after=cores_ok(), agents_after=repro.agents())
 745      rec['problems'] = block_problems(rec, rows)
 746      rec['label'] = SMOKE_LABEL if ctx['smoke'] else 'VALID' if not rec['problems'] else 'INVALID'
 747      return rec, rows
 748  
 749  
 750  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- report
 751  
 752  def heat(a, b):
 753      """(skin change degC, minutes, degC/min) between two readings."""
 754      if not (a and b) or a.get('skin') is None or b.get('skin') is None:
 755          return None
 756      mins = (b['mono'] - a['mono']) / 60
 757      return round(b['skin'] - a['skin'], 2), round(mins, 2), round((b['skin'] - a['skin']) / mins, 3) if mins else None
 758  
 759  
 760  def energy(a, b):
 761      """INDICATIVE: (charge used mAh, J at the mean voltage) from the charge counter."""
 762      try:
 763          q = (a['charge_uah'] - b['charge_uah']) / 1000
 764          return round(q, 1), round(q * 3.6 * (a['voltage_uv'] + b['voltage_uv']) / 2e6, 0)
 765      except (KeyError, TypeError):
 766          return None
 767  
 768  
 769  def stat(xs, nd=0):
 770      xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
 771      return f'median {round(mem1.med(xs), nd)} p95 {round(mem1.p95(xs), nd)} max {round(max(xs), nd)} (n {len(xs)})' \
 772          if xs else 'n 0'
 773  
 774  
 775  def mem_text(c):
 776      if not c:
 777          return '-'
 778      parts = [f'{n[4:]} Pss {m.get("Pss")} HWM {m.get("VmHWM")}' for n, m in c.items() if n.startswith('mem_')]
 779      return '; '.join(parts) + f'; MemAvailable {c.get("avail_kb")} kB'
 780  
 781  
 782  def reading_text(c):
 783      if not c:
 784          return '-'
 785      return (f'skin {c.get("skin")} status {c.get("status")} caps {c.get("caps")} battery {c.get("battery_pct")} % '
 786              f'{c.get("battery_c")} degC charge {c.get("charge_uah")} uAh')
 787  
 788  
 789  def detail(rec, rows):
 790      k = rec['block']
 791      L = []
 792      dec = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision' and 'error' not in r]
 793      if dec:
 794          L += [f'  decisions {len(dec)}: wall s {stat([r["wall_s"] for r in dec], 3)}',
 795                f'    excl. b2 /tokenize checks: {stat([r["wall_s"] - r["endpoint_s"].get("/tokenize", 0) for r in dec], 3)}',
 796                f'    reason tokens {stat([r["reason_tokens"] for r in dec])}; gen tok/s {stat([r["gen_tok_s"] for r in dec], 2)}',
 797                f'    step1 prompt_n {stat([r["step1_prompt_n"] for r in dec])} cache_n {stat([r["step1_cache_n"] for r in dec])}',
 798                f'    step2 prompt_n {stat([r["step2_prompt_n"] for r in dec])} cache_n {stat([r["step2_cache_n"] for r in dec])}',
 799                f'    restores {len([r for r in dec if "restore" in r])}: wall s '
 800                f'{stat([r["restore"]["wall_s"] for r in dec if "restore" in r], 3)}']
 801      if k == 'P0':
 802          L += [f'  {r["kind"]}: wall {r.get("wall_s")} s, step1 prompt_n {r.get("step1_prompt_n")} cache_n '
 803                f'{r.get("step1_cache_n")}, slot {r.get("erase", r.get("restore", {})).get("wall_s")} s '
 804                f'{r.get("erase", r.get("restore", {})).get("response")}' + (f' ERROR {r["error"]}' if 'error' in r else '')
 805                for r in rows]
 806      vis = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'vision' and 'error' not in r]
 807      if vis:
 808          L += [f'  vision calls {len(vis)}: wall s {stat([r["wall_s"] for r in vis], 3)}',
 809                f'    prompt ms {stat([r["prompt_ms"] for r in vis])}; prompt_n {stat([r["prompt_n"] for r in vis])}; '
 810                f'gen tok/s {stat([r["gen_tok_s"] for r in vis], 2)}']
 811      cyc = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'cycle' and 'error' not in r]
 812      if cyc:
 813          ts = [r['t'] for r in cyc]
 814          L += [f'  camera cycles {len(cyc)}: period s {stat([b - a for a, b in zip(ts, ts[1:])], 3)}; max frame age '
 815                f'{max(r["age_s"] for r in cyc):.2f} s; camera stops '
 816                f'{[r["reason"] for r in rows if r["kind"] == "camera_stop"]}'] + \
 817               [f'    detect {z} ms {stat([r["detect_ms"] for r in cyc if r["size"] == z])}' for z in (320, 640)]
 818      if 'camera' in rec:
 819          L.append(f'  camera start {rec["camera"]["start_s"]} s, affinity start {rec["camera"]["affinity_start"]}; '
 820                   f'stop {rec.get("camera_stop")}')
 821      if 'sampler' in rec:
 822          s = rec['sampler']
 823          L.append(f'  sampler: {len(s["samples"])} samples, root s {stat([x["root_s"] for x in s["samples"]], 2)}, '
 824                   f'sched {s["sched"]}, error {s["error"]}')
 825      if 'watch' in rec:
 826          w = rec['watch']
 827          L.append(f'  watch: {len(w["samples"])} scans, hits {sum(len(x["hits"]) for x in w["samples"])}, '
 828                   f'sched {w["sched"]}')
 829      if 'phases' in rec:
 830          L.append('  overrun s: ' + ', '.join(f'{p["phase"]} {p["overrun_s"]}' for p in rec['phases']))
 831      if 'warm' in rec:
 832          L.append(f'  load {rec.get("load_s")} s; warm prefix {rec["warm"]["prefix_tokens"]} tokens (prompt_n '
 833                   f'{rec["warm"]["prompt_n"]}), save {rec["warm"]["save"]["wall_s"]} s')
 834      if 'exit_code' in rec:
 835          L.append(f'  server exit {rec["exit_code"]}, SIGKILL {rec["killed"]}' +
 836                   ('' if rec['exit_code'] == 0 and not rec['killed'] else '  FINDING (#145)'))
 837      return L
 838  
 839  
 840  def report(run, recs, rows):
 841      b4_sha = run['definition']['files'].get(str(Path(__file__).resolve().relative_to(REPO)), '?')
 842      L = [f'MIND-B4 {run["utc"]}  code {b4_sha[:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 843           *(f'server {k}: {c}' for k, c in run['definition']['commands'].items()),
 844           f'launch-start skin {run["start"].get("skin")} degC (gate reference)']
 845      if run['smoke']:
 846          L.append(f'*** {SMOKE_LABEL}: {run["work_s"]} s blocks, gate before the first block only, agent/cores '
 847                   'checks not enforced ***')
 848      L += ['timings: VALID blocks only, from the owner run with no agent resident; energy INDICATIVE', '']
 849      final = {}
 850      for r in recs:
 851          if r['label'] in ('VALID', SMOKE_LABEL) or r['block'] not in final:
 852              final[r['block']] = r
 853      for k in ORDER:
 854          tries = [r for r in recs if r['block'] == k]
 855          if k not in final:
 856              L += [f'== {k}: not run', '']
 857              continue
 858          r = final[k]
 859          rr = [x for x in rows if x['block'] == k and x['session'] == r['session']]
 860          phases = [('', r.get('start'), r.get('end'))] if k != 'PY' else \
 861              [(' P phase', r.get('start'), r.get('handoff')), (' Y phase', r.get('handoff'), r.get('end'))]
 862          L.append(f'== {k}: {r["label"]} (session {r["session"]}; {len(tries)} attempt(s); gate {r.get("gate")})')
 863          L += [f'  problems: {"; ".join(r["problems"])}'] if r['problems'] else []
 864          for ph, a, b in phases:
 865              L += [f' {ph} start: {reading_text(a)}', f' {ph}   end: {reading_text(b)}',
 866                    f' {ph} heat (change, min, degC/min): {heat(a, b)}; energy (mAh, J) INDICATIVE: {energy(a, b)}',
 867                    f' {ph} RAM start: {mem_text(a)}', f' {ph} RAM   end: {mem_text(b)}']
 868          L += detail(r, rr) + ['']
 869      if 'P' in final and 'PT' in final:
 870          lab, why = comparable(final['P'], final['PT'])
 871          w = {k: mem1.med([x['wall_s'] for x in rows if x['block'] == k and x['session'] == final[k]['session']
 872                            and x['kind'] == 'decision' and 'error' not in x]) for k in ('P', 'PT')}
 873          L.append(f'P vs PT (monitoring cost): {lab}' + (f' ({"; ".join(why)})' if why else '') +
 874                   f'; median decision wall P {w["P"]} s, PT {w["PT"]} s' +
 875                   (f', PT/P {w["PT"] / w["P"]:.3f}' if w['P'] and w['PT'] else ''))
 876      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'

 881  def next_blocks(recs):
 882      """Blocks still to run, in ORDER: those without a VALID record (an INVALID one is redone)."""
 883      done = {r['block'] for r in recs if r['label'] == 'VALID'}
 884      return [k for k in ORDER if k not in done]

 887  def read_jsonl(p):
 888      return [json.loads(l) for l in p.read_text().splitlines()] if p.exists() else []
