 890  
 891  def definition(out, fr):
 892      """What fixes the blocks: SHA-256 of oneshot.sh and every imported module file under the repo (b4.py included),
 893      every server block's full command, the SHA-256 of the vision frame files."""
 894      import main  # noqa: F401  the camera code is part of the definition
 895      files = {Path(m.__file__).resolve() for m in list(sys.modules.values()) if getattr(m, '__file__', None)}
 896      files = sorted({p for p in files if REPO in p.parents} | {HERE / 'oneshot.sh'})
 897      return {'files': {str(p.relative_to(REPO)): cr.sha256(p) for p in files},
 898              'commands': {k: ' '.join(server_command(out, k)) for k in SERVER_BLOCKS},
 899              'frames': {str(i): sha((bv.FRAMES_DIR / Path(fr[i][0]['frame_file']).name).read_bytes()) for i in fr}}
 900  
 901  
 902  def definition_diff(old, new):
 903      """[(section, name, recorded, now)] for every entry that differs, is missing or is new."""
 904      return [(s, n, old.get(s, {}).get(n), new.get(s, {}).get(n)) for s in ('files', 'commands', 'frames')
 905              for n in sorted(set(old.get(s, {})) | set(new.get(s, {}))) if old.get(s, {}).get(n) != new.get(s, {}).get(n)]
 906  
 907  
 908  def launch_start():
 909      """The launch-start reading (the gate's skin reference), complete, or a clean refusal (also for --smoke),
 910      before any folder, gate, server or camera."""
 911      start = reading(light=True)
 912      if why := reading_problems(start, 'launch start'):
 913          raise SystemExit('not started: incomplete launch-start reading: ' + '; '.join(why) +
 914                           f'; su stderr {start.get("root_stderr", "")!r}')
 915      return start
 916  
 917  
 918  def session_loop(out, run, ctx, emit, blog):
 919      """Runs the remaining blocks in order; returns the end reason. Smoke: every block once, gate before the first
 920      block only, agent and cores recorded but not enforced."""
 921      todo = list(ORDER) if ctx['smoke'] else next_blocks(read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl'))
 922      for k in todo:
 923          if not ctx['smoke']:
 924              cr.wait_cores(f'block {k}')
 925              if agents := repro.agents():
 926                  return f'stopped before block {k}: agent processes running: {agents[:120]!r}'
 927          if ctx['smoke'] and k != todo[0]:
 928              g = {'passed': True, 'skipped': 'smoke: gate before the first block only'}
 929          else:
 930              g = gate(run['start']['skin'], f'block {k}')
 931          if not g['passed']:
 932              return (f'deferred before block {k}: skin {g.get("skin")} not <= {run["start"]["skin"]} + '
 933                      f'{cr.SKIN_GATE_C} degC after {g.get("waited_s")} s')
 934          pre = reading(light=True)
 935          if pre.get('status') is None or pre['status'] >= STATUS_STOP or pre.get('battery_c') is None or \
 936                  pre['battery_c'] >= BATTERY_MAX_C:
 937              return f'stopped before block {k}: status {pre.get("status")}, battery {pre.get("battery_c")} degC'
 938          if not ctx['smoke'] and not cores_ok():
 939              return f'deferred before block {k}: cores 4-7 lost after the gate'
 940          print(f'[block {k}] gate {g}', flush=True)
 941          rec, rows = run_block(k, out, ctx, emit)
 942          rec['gate'] = g
 943          blog.write(json.dumps(rec) + '\n')
 944          blog.flush()
 945          print(f'[block {k}] {rec["label"]}' + (f': {"; ".join(rec["problems"])}' if rec['problems'] else '') +
 946                f'; {len(rows)} rows', flush=True)
 947          if rec.get('interrupted'):  # a signal: the record is kept, then the run stops
 948              raise SystemExit(f'interrupted in block {k}')
 949          if rec['label'] == 'INVALID':
 950              return f'block {k} INVALID: redo in a later session (--resume {out.name})'
 951      return 'all blocks done' if ctx['smoke'] or not next_blocks(read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl')) else 'blocks remain'
 952  
 953  
 954  def main(argv=None):
 955      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
 956      ap.add_argument('--smoke', action='store_true')
 957      ap.add_argument('--resume', metavar='RUN')
 958      a = ap.parse_args(argv)
 959      if a.smoke and a.resume:
 960          ap.error('--smoke runs alone')
 961      cr.SKIN_GATE_C = SKIN_MATCH_C  # pm.thermal_gate reads it (MEM-1)
 962      if not a.smoke:
 963          pm.GATE_MAX_S = GATE_MAX_S
 964      if not a.smoke:
 965          cr.require_native()
 966          if why := cm.refusals():
 967              raise SystemExit('not started: ' + '; '.join(why))
 968      for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
 969          signal.signal(sig, cr.exit_on_signal)
 970      test, _ = b2.load_set(bc.TEST_SET, b2.TEST_SHA256, custody=False)
 971      fr = frames()
 972      print('checking server, model and projector SHA-256 (about 30 s)', flush=True)
 973      bad = [f'{p}: SHA-256 != pinned {h[:12]}' for p, h in bc.PINS.items() if not Path(p).is_file() or cr.sha256(p) != h]
 974      bad += vis3d.env_problems() + ([] if b0.repro.port_free() else ['port 8080 in use'])
 975      if bad:
 976          raise SystemExit('refused: ' + '; '.join(bad))
 977      if a.resume:
 978          out = OUT_ROOT / a.resume
 979          if Path(a.resume).name != a.resume or not (out / 'run.json').is_file():
 980              raise SystemExit(f'not resumed: {out} is not a run folder')
 981          run = json.loads((out / 'run.json').read_text())
 982          if run['smoke']:
 983              raise SystemExit('not resumed: a smoke run')
 984          if diff := definition_diff(run['definition'], definition(out, fr)):
 985              raise SystemExit(f'not resumed: the definition differs from the run start: {diff}')
 986          if not next_blocks(read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl')):
 987              raise SystemExit('not resumed: every block already VALID')
 988      else:
 989          start = launch_start()
 990          utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 991          out = OUT_ROOT / (utc + ('_smoke' if a.smoke else ''))
 992          out.mkdir(parents=True)  # a new folder; an existing one raises
 993          (out / 'slots').mkdir()
 994          run = {'utc': utc, 'smoke': a.smoke, 'work_s': SMOKE_S if a.smoke else BLOCK_S, 'start': start,
 995                 'pins': bc.PINS, 'definition': definition(out, fr),
 996                 'git_head': bc.run_out('git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD').strip(),
 997                 'test_sha256': b2.TEST_SHA256, 'items': list(ITEMS), 'frame_ids': list(FRAME_IDS),
 998                 'order': list(ORDER), 'restore_every': RESTORE_EVERY, 'sample_s': SAMPLE_S, 'watch_s': WATCH_S,
 999                 'overrun_max_s': OVERRUN_MAX_S, 'no_new_max': NO_NEW_MAX, 'request_timeout_s': REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S,
1000                 'skin_gate_c': SKIN_MATCH_C, 'gate_max_s': pm.GATE_MAX_S, 'battery_match_pct': BATTERY_MATCH_PCT}
1001          (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
1002      k = len(list(out.glob('session??.json'))) + 1
1003      with open(out / f'session{k:02d}.json', 'x') as fh:
1004          fh.write(json.dumps({'session': k, 'started': cr.utc(), 'logger_sched': mem1.logger_sched(THERMAL_LOG)},
1005                              indent=1) + '\n')
1006      ctx = {'session': k, 'smoke': a.smoke, 'dur': run['work_s'], 'test': test, 'frames': fr}
1007      print(f'run folder {out}; session {k}', flush=True)
1008      reason = None
1009      try:
1010          with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'a') as rq, open(out / 'blocks.jsonl', 'a') as blog:
1011              def emit(row):
1012                  rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
1013                  rq.flush()
1014              reason = session_loop(out, run, ctx, emit, blog)
1015      finally:
1016          for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
1017              signal.signal(sig, signal.SIG_IGN)
1018          reason = reason or f'ended by {sys.exc_info()[1]!r}'[:300]
1019          with open(out / f'session{k:02d}_end.json', 'x') as fh:
1020              fh.write(json.dumps({'session': k, 'ended': cr.utc(), 'end_reason': reason,
1021                                   'agents_end': repro.agents(), 'port_free_end': b0.repro.port_free()}, indent=1) + '\n')
1022          text = report(run, read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl'), read_jsonl(out / 'requests.jsonl'))
1023          (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
1024          (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{cr.sha256(p)}  {p.relative_to(out)}\n'
1025                                                        for p in sorted(out.rglob('*'))
1026                                                        if p.is_file() and p.name != 'artifacts.sha256'))
1027          print(text + f'\nsession {k}: {reason}\n{out}', flush=True)
1028  
1029  
1030  if __name__ == '__main__':
1031      main()
END
BEGIN heat_control.py (345 lines)
   1  """Mission heat control (DECISIONS #127 rules, #129 planned pauses, adaptive 37/35 degC schedule from #132).
   2  
   3  HeatMonitor reads the phone through root (native Termux only; `su` is not available in proot).
   4  decide() is pure and unit-tested. HeatControl ties them to the mission loop and writes the thermal trace.
   5  Code marked "copied from" is the proven campaign code, copied and trimmed (benchmark/ is not deployed).
   6  """
   7  import collections
   8  import json
   9  import math
  10  import os
  11  import queue
  12  import re
  13  import subprocess
  14  import threading
  15  import time
  16  
  17  DUMP_PERIOD_S = 5          # Android thermal status + VIRTUAL-SKIN (dumpsys)
  18  FAST_PERIOD_S = 1          # CPU zones + battery (persistent root shell)
  19  SKIN_PAUSE_C = 37.0        # ACTIVE -> PAUSE at skin >= this ...
  20  SKIN_RESUME_C = 35.0       # ... PAUSE -> ACTIVE at skin <= this
  21  MIN_ACTIVE_S = 60          # counted from camera start, including it
  22  MIN_PAUSE_S = 30           # counted from stop_camera() returning successfully
  23  STATUS_SEVERE = 3
  24  STATUS_CRITICAL = 4
  25  STATUS_RESUME = 2          # MODERATE: a CRITICAL hold ends at status <= this
  26  BATTERY_STOP_C = 45.0
  27  CPU_FAULT_C = 110.0
  28  CPU_FAULT_N = 3            # consecutive 1 s samples
  29  SKIN_STALE_S = 60          # older than this (strictly) = fail closed
  30  FAST_STALE_S = 5
  31  PAUSE_FORWARD_SPEED = 100  # UNCALIBRATED: FORWARD speed in sensor-only pause cycles
  32  DUMP_TIMEOUT_S = 10
  33  START_WAIT_S = 15          # first complete reading must arrive within this, or the mission does not start
  34  
  35  THERMAL = '/sys/class/thermal'
  36  CPU_TYPES = ('BIG', 'MID', 'LITTLE')
  37  SKIN = 'VIRTUAL-SKIN'
  38  BATTERY_TEMP = '/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'  # tenths of degC; not a thermal zone (zone number unconfirmed)
  39  TRACE_DIR = '/data/data/com.termux/files/home/robot_runs'
  40  
  41  
  42  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: root() (DECISIONS #123 form), trimmed (no thread-local stderr).
  43  def root(cmd, tag, timeout=60):
  44      """su -c "<cmd> </dev/null >FILE 2>&1", then the file is read. Returns (rc, text)."""
  45      path = f'/data/local/tmp/heat_control_{tag}.txt'
  46      r = subprocess.run(['su', '-c', f'{{ {cmd}; }} </dev/null >{path} 2>&1; rc=$?; cat {path}; exit $rc'],
  47                         stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
  48      return r.returncode, r.stdout
  49  
  50  
  51  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: to_int().
  52  def to_int(v):
  53      try:
  54          return int(v)
  55      except (TypeError, ValueError):
  56          return None
  57  
  58  
  59  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: RootShell (END marker renamed, last_stderr dropped).
  60  class RootShell:
  61      """One persistent root shell for the sysfs reads (no new su per sample). Android service calls do not go
  62      through it: they use root() (DECISIONS #123)."""
  63      END = '__heat_control_end_'
  64  
  65      def __init__(self):
  66          self.p = subprocess.Popen(['su'], stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
  67                                    bufsize=1, start_new_session=True)
  68          self.lines, self.errors, self.n = queue.Queue(), queue.Queue(), 0
  69          threading.Thread(target=self._pump, daemon=True).start()
  70          threading.Thread(target=self._pump, args=(self.p.stderr, self.errors), daemon=True).start()
  71  
  72      def _pump(self, stream=None, lines=None):
  73          stream = self.p.stdout if stream is None else stream
  74          lines = self.lines if lines is None else lines
  75          for line in stream:
  76              lines.put(line.rstrip('\n'))
  77          lines.put(None)
  78  
  79      def run(self, script, timeout=5):
  80          """Output lines of script; raises RuntimeError on timeout or a dead shell."""
  81          self.n += 1
  82          end = f'{self.END}{self.n}__'
  83          try:
  84              self.p.stdin.write(f'{script}\necho {end}\necho {end} >&2\n')
  85              self.p.stdin.flush()
  86          except OSError as e:
  87              raise RuntimeError(f'root shell: {e}') from e
  88          deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
  89          out = self._until(self.lines, end, deadline, timeout)
  90          self._until(self.errors, end, deadline, timeout)
  91          return out
  92  
  93      def _until(self, lines, end, deadline, timeout):
  94          out = []
  95          while True:
  96              try:
  97                  line = lines.get(timeout=max(0.01, deadline - time.monotonic()))
  98              except queue.Empty:
  99                  raise RuntimeError(f'root shell: no answer in {timeout} s') from None
 100              if line is None:
 101                  raise RuntimeError(f'root shell exited (rc {self.p.poll()})')
 102              if line == end:
 103                  return out
 104              if line.startswith(self.END):  # the end of an earlier command that timed out: its output is not ours
 105                  out = []
 106                  continue
 107              out.append(line)
 108  
 109      def close(self):
 110          try:
 111              self.p.stdin.close()
 112          except OSError:
 113              pass
 114          try:
 115              self.p.wait(timeout=5)
 116          except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 117              self.p.kill()
 118              self.p.wait()
 119  
 120  
 121  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: discover(), cut down to the CPU zones (never hardcoded numbers).
 122  def discover(shell):
 123      """The BIG/MID/LITTLE thermal zone numbers (lowest zone of each type)."""
 124      script = (f'for f in {THERMAL}/thermal_zone*/type; do [ -e "$f" ] || continue; '
 125                "v=; read -r v 2>/dev/null <\"$f\"; printf '%s\\t%s\\n' \"$f\" \"$v\"; done")
 126      zones = {}
 127      for line in shell.run(script, timeout=30):
 128          path, _, v = line.partition('\t')
 129          if m := re.fullmatch(re.escape(THERMAL) + r'/thermal_zone(\d+)/type', path):
 130              zones[int(m.group(1))] = v
 131      cpu = {t: next((i for i in sorted(zones) if zones[i] == t), None) for t in CPU_TYPES}
 132      if None in cpu.values():
 133          raise RuntimeError(f'CPU thermal zones by type: {cpu}; heat control not started')
 134      return cpu
 135  
 136  
 137  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: fast_sample(), cut down to CPU zones + battery.
 138  def fast_sample(shell, paths):
 139      """One 1 s reading: CPU zone files then the battery file. Raises RuntimeError unless every value was read."""
 140      lines = shell.run(f'for f in {" ".join(paths)}; do v=; read -r v 2>/dev/null <"$f"; echo "$v"; done')
 141      values = [to_int(v) for v in lines]
 142      if len(values) != len(paths) or None in values:
 143          raise RuntimeError(f'incomplete sample: {lines!r}')
 144      return {'t': time.monotonic(), 'cpu_c': max(values[:-1]) / 1000, 'bat_c': values[-1] / 10}
 145  
 146  
 147  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: TEMP_RE and parse_dump(), trimmed to status and VIRTUAL-SKIN.
 148  TEMP_RE = re.compile(r'Temperature\{mValue=([^,]+), mType=(-?\d+), mName=([^,]+), mStatus=(\d+)\}')
 149  
 150  
 151  def parse_dump(text):
 152      """Android thermal status and VIRTUAL-SKIN from 'Current temperatures from HAL' (None when absent or NaN)."""
 153      status = re.search(r'^Thermal Status: (\d+)\s*$', text, re.M)
 154      sections = {}
 155      for m in re.finditer(r'^(\S[^\n]*):\n((?:[ \t]+[^\n]*\n?)*)', text, re.M):
 156          sections[m.group(1).strip()] = m.group(2)
 157      hal = {}
 158      for m in TEMP_RE.finditer(sections.get('Current temperatures from HAL', '')):
 159          try:
 160              v = float(m.group(1))
 161          except ValueError:
 162              continue
 163          if not math.isnan(v):
 164              hal[m.group(3)] = v
 165      return {'status': int(status.group(1)) if status else None, 'skin': hal.get(SKIN)}
 166  
 167  
 168  # Copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: read_dump(), timeout 10 s.
 169  def read_dump():
 170      """`dumpsys thermalservice` (DECISIONS #123 form); t = monotonic s when the reading was in hand."""
 171      try:
 172          rc, text = root('dumpsys thermalservice', 'thermalservice', timeout=DUMP_TIMEOUT_S)
 173          d = {**parse_dump(text), 'error': None if rc == 0 else f'rc {rc}'}
 174      except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
 175          d = {'status': None, 'skin': None, 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'}
 176      return {'t': time.monotonic(), **d}
 177  
 178  
 179  class HeatMonitor:
 180      """Real hardware reader: one daemon thread, CPU zones + battery every 1 s, dumpsys every 5 s.
 181      Only complete readings are published; a failed read leaves the last one to go stale."""
 182  
 183      def __init__(self):
 184          self.on_dump = None  # HeatControl sets it; called (in this thread) with every dumpsys read
 185          self.lock = threading.Lock()
 186          self.dump = None
 187          self.fast = collections.deque(maxlen=CPU_FAULT_N)
 188          self.stopping = threading.Event()
 189          self.shell = self.thread = None
 190  
 191      def start(self):
 192          """Raises (mission not started) without root, CPU zones, or a complete first reading within 15 s."""
 193          self.shell = RootShell()
 194          try:
 195              self.paths = [f'{THERMAL}/thermal_zone{i}/temp' for i in discover(self.shell).values()] + [BATTERY_TEMP]
 196              self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._loop, daemon=True)
 197              self.thread.start()
 198              deadline = time.monotonic() + START_WAIT_S
 199              while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 200                  r = self.readings()
 201                  if r['dump'] and r['fast']:
 202                      return
 203                  time.sleep(0.2)
 204              raise RuntimeError(f'no complete heat reading (status, skin, CPU zones, battery) in {START_WAIT_S} s')
 205          except BaseException:
 206              self.stop()
 207              raise
 208  
 209      def _loop(self):
 210          next_dump = 0.0
 211          while not self.stopping.is_set():
 212              began = time.monotonic()
 213              try:
 214                  s = fast_sample(self.shell, self.paths)
 215                  with self.lock:
 216                      self.fast.append(s)
 217              except Exception:
 218                  pass  # stays stale; decide() fails closed after FAST_STALE_S
 219              if began >= next_dump:
 220                  next_dump = began + DUMP_PERIOD_S
 221                  d = read_dump()
 222                  if d['status'] is not None and d['skin'] is not None:
 223                      with self.lock:
 224                          self.dump = d
 225                  try:
 226                      if self.on_dump:
 227                          self.on_dump(d)
 228                  except Exception:
 229                      pass
 230              self.stopping.wait(max(0.0, began + FAST_PERIOD_S - time.monotonic()))
 231  
 232      def readings(self):
 233          with self.lock:
 234              return {'dump': self.dump, 'fast': list(self.fast)}
 235  
 236      def stop(self):
 237          self.stopping.set()
 238          if self.thread:
 239              self.thread.join(timeout=DUMP_TIMEOUT_S + 5)
 240          if self.shell:
 241              self.shell.close()
 242  
 243  
 244  def decide(now, readings, state):
 245      """(target, reason): target ACTIVE / PAUSE / HOLD / END. Pure; times are monotonic s.
 246      readings: {'dump': {'t', 'status', 'skin'} or None, 'fast': [{'t', 'cpu_c', 'bat_c'}, ...] oldest first}.
 247      state: {'phase': ACTIVE / PAUSE / HOLD, 'since': start of the phase (a HOLD keeps its pause's start)}.
 248      Staleness: a reading exactly SKIN_STALE_S / FAST_STALE_S old is still fresh; older ends the mission."""
 249      # Precedence and staleness copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: block_limit(); fail-closed first.
 250      dump, fast = readings['dump'], readings['fast']
 251      if dump is None or now - dump['t'] > SKIN_STALE_S:
 252          return 'END', f'fail-closed: no Android status + {SKIN} reading for > {SKIN_STALE_S} s'
 253      if not fast or now - fast[-1]['t'] > FAST_STALE_S:
 254          return 'END', f'fail-closed: no CPU zone + battery reading for > {FAST_STALE_S} s'
 255      if fast[-1]['bat_c'] >= BATTERY_STOP_C:
 256          return 'END', f'battery {fast[-1]["bat_c"]:.1f} >= {BATTERY_STOP_C} degC'
 257      if len(fast) >= CPU_FAULT_N and all(s['cpu_c'] >= CPU_FAULT_C for s in fast[-CPU_FAULT_N:]):
 258          return 'END', (f'CPU zone >= {CPU_FAULT_C:.0f} degC in {CPU_FAULT_N} consecutive 1 s samples '
 259                         f'({[s["cpu_c"] for s in fast[-CPU_FAULT_N:]]})')
 260      status, skin, phase = dump['status'], dump['skin'], state['phase']
 261      if status >= STATUS_CRITICAL or (phase == 'HOLD' and status > STATUS_RESUME):
 262          return 'HOLD', f'Android thermal status {status}: motors stopped until status <= {STATUS_RESUME}'
 263      note = (f'Android thermal status SEVERE ({status}): no optional Gemma work in the mission loop, nothing changed'
 264              if status == STATUS_SEVERE else '')
 265      # Adaptive rule copied from benchmark/campaign/phase23.py: switch() ('adaptive').
 266      elapsed = now - state['since']
 267      if phase == 'ACTIVE':
 268          if skin >= SKIN_PAUSE_C and elapsed >= MIN_ACTIVE_S:
 269              return 'PAUSE', f'{SKIN} {skin:.2f} >= {SKIN_PAUSE_C} after {elapsed:.0f} s active'
 270          return 'ACTIVE', note
 271      if skin <= SKIN_RESUME_C and elapsed >= MIN_PAUSE_S:
 272          return 'ACTIVE', f'{SKIN} {skin:.2f} <= {SKIN_RESUME_C} after {elapsed:.0f} s paused'
 273      return 'PAUSE', note
 274  
 275  
 276  class HeatControl:
 277      """The mission's heat controller: monitor readings -> decide(), the phase state, and the thermal trace."""
 278  
 279      def __init__(self, monitor, trace_dir=TRACE_DIR, clock=time.monotonic):
 280          self.monitor, self.trace_dir, self.clock = monitor, trace_dir, clock
 281          self.state = {'phase': 'ACTIVE', 'since': None}
 282          self.note = ''
 283          self.distance = None
 284          self.trace_path = None
 285          self.lock = threading.Lock()
 286          monitor.on_dump = lambda d: self.event('dumpsys', d['error'], d)
 287  
 288      @property
 289      def phase(self):
 290          return self.state['phase']
 291  
 292      def now(self):
 293          return self.clock()
 294  
 295      def start(self):
 296          """Starts the monitor (raises if it cannot) and begins ACTIVE; call just before the camera starts."""
 297          self.trace_path = os.path.join(self.trace_dir, time.strftime('heat_trace_%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ.jsonl', time.gmtime()))
 298          try:
 299              os.makedirs(self.trace_dir, exist_ok=True)
 300          except OSError as e:
 301              print(f'[HEAT] trace folder failed: {e}')
 302          print(f'[HEAT] trace: {self.trace_path}')
 303          self.monitor.start()
 304          self.state = {'phase': 'ACTIVE', 'since': self.clock()}
 305          self.event('start')
 306  
 307      def stop(self):
 308          self.monitor.stop()
 309  
 310      def decide(self, distance=None):
 311          self.distance = distance
 312          target, reason = decide(self.clock(), self.monitor.readings(), self.state)
 313          if target == 'END':
 314              self.event('end', reason)
 315          elif target == self.phase and reason != self.note:
 316              self.note = reason
 317              if reason:
 318                  print(f'[HEAT] {reason}')
 319                  self.event('note', reason)
 320          return target, reason
 321  
 322      def enter(self, phase, since=None):
 323          """Phase change; since=None keeps the current phase start (PAUSE <-> HOLD)."""
 324          old = self.phase
 325          self.state = {'phase': phase, 'since': self.state['since'] if since is None else since}
 326          self.note = ''
 327          self.event('hold_start' if phase == 'HOLD' else 'hold_end' if old == 'HOLD' else 'phase_change')
 328  
 329      def summary(self):
 330          d = self.monitor.readings()['dump'] or {}
 331          return f"skin {d.get('skin')} degC status {d.get('status')}"
 332  
 333      def event(self, name, detail=None, dump=None):
 334          """One JSON line in the trace; a write failure is printed, never raised."""
 335          r = self.monitor.readings()
 336          d, fast = dump or r['dump'] or {}, r['fast'][-1] if r['fast'] else {}
 337          row = {'t_mono': self.clock(), 't_utc': time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), 'event': name,
 338                 'phase': self.phase, 'status': d.get('status'), 'skin_c': d.get('skin'),
 339                 'battery_c': fast.get('bat_c'), 'cpu_max_c': fast.get('cpu_c'), 'distance_cm': self.distance,
 340                 'detail': detail}
 341          try:
 342              with self.lock, open(self.trace_path, 'a') as f:
 343                  f.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 344          except (OSError, TypeError) as e:
 345              print(f'[HEAT] trace write failed: {e}')
END
BEGIN run_mission.py (118 lines)
   1  """Autonomous entry point. --dry uses RobotCam without opening motor USB.
   2  --no-picker: today's rules (incl. person approach) and person-stop; neither the Gemma client nor M2 is loaded."""
   3  import signal
   4  import sys
   5  import time
   6  
   7  sys.path.insert(0, '/data/data/com.termux/files/home/robot')
   8  import main as R
   9  import heat_control
  10  
  11  
  12  def exit_on_signal(signum, frame):
  13      raise SystemExit(128 + signum)
  14  
  15  
  16  def build_picker():
  17      """(Picker, None), or (None, reason) when it cannot run: the mission then continues in EXPLORE without picks
  18      (a fallback, never a stop). The owner starts the server with setup_q4 first."""
  19      import picker
  20      import relate
  21      try:
  22          gemma = picker.Gemma()
  23      except Exception as exc:
  24          return None, f'llama-server at {picker.URL} not ready ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}); run setup_q4 first'
  25      try:
  26          return picker.Picker(gemma, relate.Relate()), None
  27      except Exception as exc:
  28          return None, f'M2 not available ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})'
  29  
  30  
  31  def main(argv=None):
  32      argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
  33      dry = '--dry' in argv
  34      camera_source = 'termux_photo' if '--termux-photo' in argv else 'robotcam'
  35      person_search = '--person-search' in argv
  36      use_picker = '--no-picker' not in argv
  37      args = [a for a in argv if not a.startswith('--')]
  38      mission = args[0] if args else 'explore and map the rooms'
  39  
  40      signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, exit_on_signal)
  41      signal.signal(signal.SIGHUP, exit_on_signal)
  42      motors = None
  43      robot = None
  44      heat = None
  45      pick = None
  46      try:
  47          # Before the motor reader and heat threads exist, so the ORT thread diff sees only ORT workers;
  48          # raises (mission not started) when the workers or their affinity cannot be verified.
  49          R.get_detector(pinned=True)
  50          # Also before those threads: Relate's ORT thread diff must see only its own worker.
  51          pick, picker_off = build_picker() if use_picker else (None, '--no-picker')
  52          if use_picker and pick is None:
  53              print(f'[PICKER] disabled: {picker_off} — the mission continues in EXPLORE')
  54          if not dry:
  55              from motors import Motors
  56              motors = Motors()
  57              motors.connect()
  58              time.sleep(1)
  59              print('PING:', 'ALIVE' if motors.ping() else 'NO REPLY')
  60              print('distance:', motors.get_distance(), 'cm')
  61          robot = R.Robot(motors, camera_source=camera_source)
  62          if person_search:
  63              robot.size_policy.set_mode('person_search')
  64          if use_picker:
  65              robot.picker = pick
  66              robot.set_mode('EXPLORE', 'mission start')
  67          heat = heat_control.HeatControl(heat_control.HeatMonitor())
  68          heat.start()  # raises without root or a first complete reading: mission not started
  69          heat.event('picker_config', {'enabled': True, **pick.config()} if pick else {'enabled': False, 'reason': picker_off})
  70          robot.start_camera(heat)
  71          if not dry:
  72              R.warm_up()
  73          print(f"\nmission: {mission}{'   [DRY — no motor commands]' if dry else ''}")
  74          print('ctrl-C to stop\n')
  75          robot.run_mission(mission, heat=heat)
  76      except KeyboardInterrupt:
  77          print('\ninterrupted')
  78      finally:
  79          for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
  80              signal.signal(signum, signal.SIG_IGN)
  81          cleanup_errors = []
  82          if motors:
  83              try:
  84                  motors.stop()
  85              except Exception as exc:
  86                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[MOTOR] stop failed: {exc}')
  87          if robot:
  88              try:
  89                  robot.stop_camera()
  90              except Exception as exc:
  91                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[CAM] stop failed: {exc}')
  92          if pick:
  93              if not pick.stop():
  94                  cleanup_errors.append('[PICKER] worker still running after its timeout')
  95              if robot:
  96                  try:
  97                      robot.collect_pick()  # trace a pick that ended during shutdown
  98                  except Exception as exc:
  99                      cleanup_errors.append(f'[PICKER] last pick not traced: {exc}')
 100          if heat:
 101              try:
 102                  heat.stop()
 103              except Exception as exc:
 104                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[HEAT] stop failed: {exc}')
 105          if motors:
 106              try:
 107                  motors.disconnect()
 108              except Exception as exc:
 109                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[MOTOR] disconnect failed: {exc}')
 110          for error in cleanup_errors:
 111              print(error)
 112          if robot:
 113              print(f'rooms: {robot.known_rooms}')
 114              print(f'moves: {robot.last_moves}')
 115  
 116  
 117  if __name__ == '__main__':
 118      main()
END

BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 222-254)
 222      def start_camera(self, heat=None):
 223          """heat: the mission's HeatControl, for the detector trace lines (None: no trace)."""
 224          created = self.detector is None
 225          # Shared by detect_scene and the robot loop. MID cores (#128); raises before `am start` when the
 226          # ORT workers or an affinity cannot be verified, so the mission does not start.
 227          self.detector = get_detector(pinned=True)
 228          if created and heat:
 229              heat.event('detector_config', {**self.detector.config(), 'camera_rate': CAMERA_RATE})
 230          # Affinity is a power setting, not a safety stop: a failed re-pin is logged and the mission continues.
 231          affinity = self.detector.check_affinity()
 232          if not affinity['ok']:
 233              print(f"[DETECTOR] affinity re-pin failed: {affinity['errors']} — continuing")
 234          if heat:
 235              heat.event('detector_affinity', affinity)
 236          self.slot = new_slot()
 237          self.slot_n += 1
 238          self.last_640 = None
 239          if self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
 240              self.camera_session = None
 241              self.camera_frame = None
 242              started_boot_s = time.clock_gettime(time.CLOCK_BOOTTIME)
 243              subprocess.run(['am', 'start', '-n', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam/.StartActivity',
 244                              '--es', 'mode', 'B', '--ei', 'rate', str(CAMERA_RATE)],
 245                             check=True, timeout=10, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
 246                             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
 247              deadline = time.monotonic() + 8
 248              while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 249                  result = read_frame(min_capture_boot_s=started_boot_s)
 250                  if result['status'] == 'ok':
 251                      self.camera_session = result['session']
 252                      return
 253                  time.sleep(0.1)
 254              raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
END

 258              subprocess.run(['am', 'broadcast', '-n', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam/.ControlReceiver',
 259                              '-a', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam.STOP'],
 260                             check=True, timeout=5, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
 261                             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 263-280)
 263      def move(self, cmd, duration=CYCLE_MOVE_TIME, speed=MOTOR_SPEED):
 264          print(f'  [MOTOR] {cmd}')
 265          if self.motors:
 266              if cmd == 'FORWARD':  self.motors.forward(speed, duration)
 267              elif cmd == 'LEFT':   self.motors.rotate_left(120, duration)
 268              elif cmd == 'RIGHT':  self.motors.rotate_right(120, duration)
 269              elif cmd == 'BACK':   self.motors.backward(MOTOR_SPEED, duration)
 270              elif cmd == 'STOP':   self.motors.stop()
 271              elif cmd == 'HOLD':   # stand still for the cycle; the mission continues (GREET, person-stop)
 272                  self.motors.stop()
 273                  time.sleep(duration)
 274              elif cmd == 'STRAFE_RIGHT': self.motors.strafe_right(100, duration)
 275              elif cmd == 'STRAFE_LEFT':  self.motors.strafe_left(100, duration)
 276          else:
 277              time.sleep(duration)
 278          self.last_moves.append(cmd)
 279          if len(self.last_moves) > 10:
 280              self.last_moves.pop(0)
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 282-292)
 282      def get_distance(self):
 283          """cm ahead. 999 = no usable reading (treated as clear).
 284          Firmware sends -1 for no echo, i.e. nothing within ~4m."""
 285          if not self.motors:
 286              return 999
 287          d = self.motors.get_distance()
 288          if time.time() - self.motors.state.get("dist_at", 0) > 1.0:
 289              return 999          # stale or never received
 290          if d < 0:
 291              return 400          # no echo = clear to sensor max range
 292          return d
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 383-462)
 383      def run_cycle(self, photo_path=None):
 384          self.cycle += 1
 385          self.slot['cycles'] += 1
 386          self.collect_pick()  # a pick that finished meanwhile changes the mode from this cycle on
 387          age = None
 388          distance = self.get_distance()
 389          print(f'\n── Cycle {self.cycle} | {self.heat_text} | dist:{distance}cm ──')
 390  
 391          # Vision: every cycle must have a fresh usable frame before any movement.
 392          if photo_path:
 393              path = photo_path
 394          elif self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
 395              frame_result = read_frame(session=self.camera_session)
 396              if frame_result['status'] != 'ok' or (self.camera_frame is not None and
 397                      frame_result['frame'] <= self.camera_frame):
 398                  reason = frame_result['status'] if frame_result['status'] != 'ok' else 'no new'
 399                  print(f'  [CAM] {reason} frame — stop')
 400                  self.slot['camera_stops'][reason] = self.slot['camera_stops'].get(reason, 0) + 1
 401                  if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 402                  return 'STOP'
 403              self.camera_session = frame_result['session']
 404              self.camera_frame = frame_result['frame']
 405              frame = frame_result['image']
 406              age = frame_result['age_s']
 407              self.slot['frames'] += 1
 408              self.slot['age_s'].append(age)
 409          else:
 410              if not take_photo(PHOTO_A):
 411                  print('  [CAM] Photo failed')
 412                  if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 413                  return 'STOP'
 414              path = PHOTO_A
 415  
 416          detections = []
 417          try:
 418              if photo_path:
 419                  results = detect_scene(path)
 420              else:
 421                  if self.detector is None: self.detector = get_detector()
 422                  size = self.size_policy.next_size()
 423                  if self.camera_source == 'termux_photo':
 424                      from PIL import Image, ImageOps
 425                      with Image.open(path) as source:
 426                          frame = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert('RGB')
 427                  started = time.perf_counter()
 428                  detections = self.detector.detect(frame, size)
 429                  detect_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
 430                  self.slot['detect_ms'][size].append(detect_ms)
 431                  self.size_policy.observe(detections, frame.height)
 432                  results = scene_from_detections(detections, *frame.size)
 433                  if size == 640:
 434                      self.last_640 = (frame, detections)
 435                  print(f'  [YOLO] size={size} person={any(d["class_name"] == "person" for d in detections)} '
 436                        f'detect={detect_ms:.0f}ms age={"n/a" if age is None else f"{age:.2f}s"}')
 437          except Exception as exc:
 438              print(f'  [VISION] {exc} — stop')
 439              if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 440              return 'STOP'
 441          scene = scene_to_text(results)
 442          self.scene_log.append(scene)
 443          if len(self.scene_log) > 10:
 444              self.scene_log.pop(0)
 445          print(f'  [YOLO] {scene}')
 446  
 447          # Fast navigation rules, shaped by the mode; person-stop last. The legacy tuples keep one person (the most
 448          # confident); person-stop also sees every other person detection's area bucket.
 449          people = [r[3] for r in results if r[0] == 'person'] + [
 450              scene_from_detections([d], *frame.size)[0][3] for d in detections if d['class_name'] == 'person']
 451          move = self.choose_move(results, distance, people)
 452          print(f'  [NAV] {move}')
 453          self.request_pick(results, distance)  # never waits; M2 runs during the move below
 454  
 455          # Execute move (motors cool during movement)
 456          if move != 'STOP':
 457              self.move(move)
 458          else:
 459              print('  [STOP] Mission complete or waiting')
 460              self.state = 'found'
 461  
 462          return move
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 499-520)
 499      def request_pick(self, results, distance):
 500          """Start a pick in the worker when the cadence says so (owner D2); ACTIVE cycles only, never at Android
 501          status SEVERE or above (#127: no optional Gemma work), at most one in flight, only with a 640 frame."""
 502          if self.picker is None:
 503              return
 504          severe = self.severe_now()
 505          (self.picker.forbid if severe else self.picker.allow)()  # also cancels a pick already running
 506          if severe != self.picker_severe:
 507              self.picker_severe = severe
 508              print(f"  [PICKER] {'no picks at status SEVERE or above; mode stays ' + self.mode if severe else 'picks resume'}")
 509              self.trace('picker_severe', {'severe': severe, 'mode': self.mode})
 510          person = any(r[0] == 'person' for r in results)
 511          now = time.monotonic()
 512          trigger = self.cadence.due(now, person, distance < OBSTACLE_DIST,
 513                                     ready=not (self.pick_inflight or severe or self.last_640 is None))
 514          if trigger is None:
 515              return
 516          self.picker.submit({
 517              'slot': self.slot_n, 'trigger': trigger, 'eligible': picker.eligible(person), 'frame_640': self.last_640,
 518              'situation': picker.situation(self.mission, results, distance, self.mode, now - self.mode_since,
 519                                            self.last_moves, list(self.known_rooms))})
 520          self.pick_inflight = True
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 522-525)
 522      def severe_now(self):
 523          """The latest Android status is SEVERE or above (#127: no optional Gemma work)."""
 524          dump = self.heat.monitor.readings()['dump'] if self.heat else None
 525          return bool(dump) and dump['status'] >= STATUS_SEVERE
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 551-566)
 551      def pause_cycle(self):
 552          """Planned heat pause (#129): camera off, no YOLO; ultrasonic-only rules."""
 553          self.cycle += 1
 554          distance = self.get_distance()
 555          print(f'\n── Pause cycle {self.cycle} | {self.heat_text} | dist:{distance}cm ──')
 556          if distance == 999:     # stale or no reading: never drive blind, rotate in place
 557              move = getattr(self, 'avoid_side', 'LEFT')
 558          else:
 559              move = self.navigate_rules([], distance)
 560          print(f'  [NAV] {move}')
 561          if move != 'STOP':
 562              self.move(move, speed=PAUSE_FORWARD_SPEED)  # speed applies to FORWARD only
 563          else:
 564              print('  [STOP] Mission complete or waiting')
 565              self.state = 'found'
 566          return move
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 568-631)
 568      def run_mission(self, mission_text, heat=None):
 569          """Run until mission complete or stopped, under heat control (#127, #129).
 570          heat: a started heat_control.HeatControl; the camera is already on (ACTIVE)."""
 571          if heat is None:
 572              raise ValueError('run_mission needs heat control (heat_control.HeatControl)')
 573          self.mission = mission_text
 574          self.state = 'navigating'
 575          self.running = True
 576          self.heat = heat
 577          if self.picker:
 578              self.picker.allow()
 579          print(f'\n[ROBOT] Mission: {mission_text}')
 580  
 581          try:
 582              while self.running and self.state != 'found':
 583                  self.collect_pick()  # also in PAUSE/HOLD: every pick is traced when it ends
 584                  target, reason = heat.decide(self.get_distance())
 585                  if self.picker and self.severe_now():
 586                      self.picker.forbid()  # before this cycle's vision: no further picker request at SEVERE
 587                  self.heat_text = heat.summary()
 588                  if target == 'END':
 589                      print(f'[HEAT] END: {reason}')
 590                      break
 591                  if target != heat.phase:
 592                      print(f'[HEAT] {heat.phase} -> {target}: {reason}')
 593                      if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE':      # into PAUSE or HOLD: camera off, detector stays loaded
 594                          if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 595                          if self.picker: self.picker.forbid()  # no M2 or Gemma outside ACTIVE (#129)
 596                          try:
 597                              self.stop_camera()
 598                          except Exception as exc:
 599                              print(f'[CAM] stop failed: {exc} — mission ends')
 600                              heat.event('camera_stop_fail', str(exc))
 601                              break
 602                          heat.event('camera_stop_ok')
 603                          self.end_slot(heat)
 604                          heat.enter(target, since=heat.now())
 605                      elif target == 'ACTIVE':
 606                          started = heat.now()
 607                          try:
 608                              self.start_camera(heat)  # resets the frame counter, session and slot numbers
 609                          except Exception as exc:
 610                              print(f'[CAM] start failed: {exc} — mission ends')
 611                              heat.event('camera_start_fail', str(exc))
 612                              break
 613                          heat.event('camera_start_ok')
 614                          heat.enter('ACTIVE', since=started)
 615                          if self.picker: self.picker.allow()
 616                      else:                           # PAUSE <-> HOLD; camera already off
 617                          heat.enter(target)
 618  
 619                  if heat.phase == 'HOLD':
 620                      if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 621                      time.sleep(1)
 622                      continue
 623                  move = self.run_cycle() if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE' else self.pause_cycle()
 624  
 625                  if move == 'STOP':
 626                      break
 627          finally:                                # also on Ctrl-C, SIGTERM/SIGHUP (SystemExit) and errors
 628              if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE':
 629                  self.end_slot(heat)
 630          print('[ROBOT] Mission ended')
 631          if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
END
BEGIN picker.py (257 lines, excerpt lines 158-257)
 158  class Picker:
 159      """One background worker thread runs each pick: M2 (relate.Relate) on the latest 640 frame, then Gemma. It gets
 160      plain data in a request and returns a result; it never touches motors, camera, heat state or the robot. Any
 161      failure becomes an EXPLORE result with the reason (a fallback, never a stop). The mission thread calls allow()
 162      while ACTIVE and below SEVERE and forbid() otherwise; a running pick checks before M2, after M2 and before every
 163      HTTP request, and a forbid() cancels it for good (a later allow() does not revive it). Only a request already
 164      sent (<= TIMEOUT_S) or an M2 inference already started can still finish."""
 165  
 166      def __init__(self, gemma, relate):
 167          self.gemma, self.relate = gemma, relate
 168          self.requests, self.results = queue.Queue(), queue.Queue()
 169          self.allowed = threading.Event()
 170          self.generation = 0  # forbid() bumps it; a pick runs only while it is unchanged
 171          ready = queue.Queue()
 172          self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.loop, args=(ready,), daemon=True)
 173          self.thread.start()
 174          error = ready.get()
 175          if error:
 176              self.thread.join()
 177              raise error
 178  
 179      def loop(self, ready):
 180          try:
 181              self.relate.pin_caller()  # this thread runs M2: BIG 6-7, verified
 182          except BaseException as e:
 183              ready.put(e)
 184              return
 185          ready.put(None)
 186          while (request := self.requests.get()) is not None:
 187              self.results.put(self.pick(request))
 188  
 189      def allow(self):
 190          self.allowed.set()
 191  
 192      def forbid(self):
 193          if self.allowed.is_set():
 194              self.generation += 1
 195              self.allowed.clear()
 196  
 197      def pick(self, request):
 198          out = {'slot': request['slot'], 'trigger': request['trigger'],
 199                 'eligible': [MODES[m] for m in request['eligible']], 'mode': 'EXPLORE', 'probs': None,
 200                 'm2_ms': None, 'm2_boxes': None, 'm2_relations': None, 'm2_repinned': None,
 201                 'picker_ms': None, 'prompt_tokens': None, 'fallback': None, 'cancelled': False}
 202          generation = self.generation
 203  
 204          def check():
 205              if generation != self.generation or not self.allowed.is_set():
 206                  raise Cancelled('optional work not allowed (left ACTIVE, SEVERE or stop)')
 207          self.gemma.check = check
 208          stage = 'M2'
 209          try:
 210              check()
 211              affinity = self.relate.check_affinity()
 212              out['m2_repinned'] = affinity['repinned']
 213              if not affinity['ok']:
 214                  raise RuntimeError(f"affinity: {affinity['errors']}")
 215              check()  # a forbid() during the affinity check: no M2 run
 216              text, out['m2_boxes'], out['m2_relations'], out['m2_ms'] = self.relate.relations(*request['frame_640'])
 217              check()
 218              stage = 'Gemma'
 219              phrases = out['eligible']
 220              started = time.perf_counter()
 221              dist = self.gemma.decide(request['situation'] + '\n' + text, phrases)
 222              check()  # a forbid() while /completion was in flight cancels it too
 223              out['picker_ms'] = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
 224              out['probs'] = dist
 225              out['prompt_tokens'] = self.gemma.prompt_tokens
 226              choice = max(phrases, key=lambda k: dist[k])  # ties: first offered, as in ladder.py
 227              out['mode'] = request['eligible'][phrases.index(choice)]
 228          except Exception as e:
 229              out['mode'] = 'EXPLORE'
 230              out['fallback'] = f'{stage}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 231              out['cancelled'] = isinstance(e, Cancelled)
 232          return out
 233  
 234      def submit(self, request):
 235          self.requests.put(request)
 236  
 237      def result(self):
 238          """A finished pick, or None (never waits)."""
 239          try:
 240              return self.results.get_nowait()
 241          except queue.Empty:
 242              return None
 243  
 244      def stop(self, timeout=TIMEOUT_S + 5):
 245          """Cancels the running pick (after its current request) and ends the worker; True when it has ended."""
 246          self.forbid()
 247          self.requests.put(None)
 248          self.thread.join(timeout)
 249          return not self.thread.is_alive()
 250  
 251      def config(self):
 252          return {'url': URL, 'options': list(MODES.values()), 'instruction': INSTRUCTION, 'timeout_s': TIMEOUT_S,
 253                  'cadence': {'events': ['person_appeared', 'person_left', 'blocked'], 'min_gap_s': MIN_GAP_S,
 254                              'heartbeat_s': HEARTBEAT_S, 'phases': ['ACTIVE'], 'not_at_status': 'SEVERE or above'},
 255                  'm2': {'cpus': sorted(self.relate.cpus), 'threads': 2, 'worker_tids': self.relate.worker_tids,
 256                         'caller_tid': self.relate.caller_tid, 'affinity': self.relate.affinity(),
 257                         'parity': 'graph SHA-256 equals the parity-passed graph (#130)'}}
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 173-181)
 173  def get_detector(pinned=False):
 174      """Shared detector. pinned=True (the robot process) creates the PinnedDetector; later get_detector() calls,
 175      e.g. from detect_scene(), then return that same one. Without pinned it is a plain Detector(), as before."""
 176      global _detector
 177      if _detector is None:
 178          _detector = PinnedDetector() if pinned else Detector()
 179      elif pinned and not isinstance(_detector, PinnedDetector):
 180          raise RuntimeError('a default detector is already loaded; the robot needs one pinned session pair')
 181      return _detector
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 201-218)
 201  def scene_from_detections(detections, frame_width, frame_height):
 202      """Legacy tuple view used by navigation, stereo and text callers."""
 203      results = []
 204      seen = set()
 205      for d in detections:
 206          label = d['class_name']
 207          if label in seen:
 208              continue
 209          seen.add(label)
 210          x1, y1, x2, y2 = d['box_xyxy']
 211          cx, cy, w, h = (x1+x2)/2, (y1+y2)/2, x2-x1, y2-y1
 212          area = w*h/(frame_width*frame_height)
 213          pos = 'left' if cx < frame_width/3 else 'right' if cx > 2*frame_width/3 else 'center'
 214          dist = 'very close' if area>0.3 else 'close' if area>0.1 else 'medium' if area>0.03 else 'far'
 215          results.append((label, round(d['conf'], 2), pos, dist,
 216                          round(cx*640/frame_width), round(cy*640/frame_height),
 217                          round(w*640/frame_width), round(h*640/frame_height)))
 218      return results
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 123-136)
 123  def load_set(path, want, custody=True):
 124      """(set, bytes SHA-256): refused when custody and the path is inside the repo (checked before reading) or the
 125      hash differs. 'visit' in labels, current activity and allowed lists reads 'approach'."""
 126      if custody and inside_repo(path):
 127          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
 128      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
 129      if want is not None and sha(data) != want:
 130          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {want[:12]}')
 131      s = json.loads(data)
 132      ren = {'visit': 'approach'}
 133      for it in s['items']:
 134          it['current'], it['strict'] = ren.get(it['current'], it['current']), ren.get(it['strict'], it['strict'])
 135          it['allowed'], it['also_ok'] = [ren.get(n, n) for n in it['allowed']], [ren.get(n, n) for n in it['also_ok']]
 136      return s, sha(data)
END

 150                        f'Allowed actions: {", ".join(it["allowed"])}'])

 157  def tail(variant, names):
 158      words = f'{", ".join(names[:-1])} or {names[-1]}.'
 159      if variant == 'V3':
 160          return ('Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule that fits. Then write a new line '
 161                  f"starting with 'Action:' and one word: {words}")
 162      return f'Which action next? Answer with one word: {words}'

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 165-167)
 165  def messages(s, it, variant, order):
 166      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': SYSTEM[variant]},
 167              {'role': 'user', 'content': state(s, it) + '\n\n' + tail(variant, offered(it, order))}]

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 170-181)
 170  def first_ids(post, text, ids, names, sep=''):
 171      """({name: first token id after text}, problems): text's ids must stay a prefix; first tokens distinct."""
 172      out, why = {}, []
 173      for n in names:
 174          full = b0.tokenize(post, text + sep + n)
 175          if full[:len(ids)] != ids or len(full) == len(ids):
 176              why.append(f'{n!r}: prompt ids change or no token follows')
 177          else:
 178              out[n] = full[len(ids)]
 179      if len(set(out.values())) != len(out):
 180          why.append(f'first tokens not distinct: {out}')
 181      return out, why
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 184-208)
 184  def preflight(post, s, blocks, info, tag):
 185      """prompts[(variant, order, id)] = (text, ids, {name: id}); every prompt rendered and tokenized, no /completion.
 186      Problems go to info['problems']."""
 187      prompts, sizes = {}, {}
 188      for v, order in blocks:
 189          for it in s['items']:
 190              names = offered(it, order)
 191              text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, it, v, order))
 192              if v == 'V3':  # scored position checked with an empty reason; rechecked per item with the real one
 193                  t2 = text + ACTION
 194                  nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, b0.tokenize(post, t2), names, ' ')
 195              else:
 196                  nid, why = first_ids(post, text, ids, names)
 197              info['problems'] += [f'{tag} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 198              prompts[v, order, it['id']] = (text, ids, nid)
 199              sizes.setdefault(v, []).append(len(ids))
 200              info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).setdefault('V3' if v == 'V3' else 'V1/V2', {}).update(nid)
 201              if tag == 'DEV' and it['id'] == SHOW_ITEM and order == 'FWD':
 202                  info[f'prompt_{v}_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 203      for v in sizes:
 204          info.setdefault('system_tokens', {})[v] = len(b0.tokenize(post, SYSTEM[v], special=False))
 205          info.setdefault('prompt_tokens', {})[f'{tag} {v}'] = (min(sizes[v]), max(sizes[v]))
 206          if max(sizes[v]) + CTX_MARGIN > CTX:
 207              info['problems'].append(f'{tag} {v}: longest prompt {max(sizes[v])} + {CTX_MARGIN} > {CTX}')
 208      return prompts
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 211-238)
 211  def score(post, it, v, order, prompt):
 212      """One row: probabilities over the offered names (raw and renormalised), top, margin, counters; V3 adds the
 213      reason and its step-1 counters."""
 214      names, (text, ids, nid) = offered(it, order), prompt
 215      t0, row = time.perf_counter(), {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'offered': names}
 216      if v == 'V3':
 217          r1 = post('/completion', {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **REASON, 'cache_prompt': True})
 218          reason = r1['content']
 219          t2 = text + reason + ACTION
 220          ids = b0.tokenize(post, t2)
 221          nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, ids, names, ' ')
 222          if why:
 223              raise RuntimeError(f'scored position after the reason: {why}')
 224          tm1 = r1.get('timings', {})
 225          row.update(reason=reason, reason_tokens=r1.get('tokens_predicted'), reason_stop=r1.get('stop_type'),
 226                     reason_prompt_n=tm1.get('prompt_n'), reason_cache_n=tm1.get('cache_n'))
 227      body = {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}
 228      res = post('/completion', body)
 229      raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: [nid[n]] for n in names})
 230      if norm is None:
 231          raise RuntimeError(f'item {it["id"]}: an offered name is outside the top {b0.PICK["n_probs"]}: {raw}')
 232      top = max(norm, key=norm.get)  # ties: first offered
 233      second = sorted(norm.values())[-2] if len(norm) > 1 else 0.0
 234      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 235      row.update(prompt_tokens=len(ids), prompt_ids_sha256=sha(json.dumps(ids)), prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'),
 236                 cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'), wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3),
 237                 probs_raw=raw, probs=norm, top=top, margin=norm[top] - second)
 238      return row
END

  21  def good(skin=30.0, battery=90, mono=0.0, mem=('runner',)):

|---|---|---|
| R1-1 P1 camera start failure leaves capture running | `cam['on'] = True` set before every start attempt (arm start and each pause-end restart); cleanup runs `b4.stop_camera(robot)` whenever `cam['on']`; a failed restart ends the arm with the camera still owned | `run_arm`, `mission_loop`; test `Loop.test_camera_start_failure_ends_and_leaves_camera_owned` |
| R1-2 P1 work ceiling not enforced | absolute deadline `Mission.deadline`; `Mission.start` refuses at/after it; scripted offers only for k*10 s < 1800; mission loop starts no cycle after it and cuts the last move; the decision in flight may finish; overrun = max(0, after scheduler join - deadline), INVALID > 15 s; request timeout 60 s (`b4.post`) | `Mission.start/offer_due`, `mission_loop`, `run_arm`, `arm_problems`; tests `Offers`, `Schedulers.test_nothing_starts_after_the_deadline`, `Labels.test_invalid` (overrun 15.5) |
| R1-3 P1 camera failures could be VALID | any stop other than 'no new' ends the arm and is INVALID; 'no new' re-read after 0.1 s, >3 INVALID; camera start/stop failure at a heat transition INVALID | `mission_loop`, `arm_problems`; tests `Loop.test_no_new_frames_limit`, `Labels.test_invalid` |
| R1-4 P1 resume did not freeze all inputs | `definition()`: SHA-256 of every imported module under the repo (b4, bc, b2, main, heat_control, detect_person, ...), `oneshot.sh`, the B2 run of record, the TEST set, and the full server command; `--resume` refuses any `b4.definition_diff` (reused). No frames used | `definition`, `main_`; test `Sessions.test_definition_and_resume_refusal` |
| R1-5 P1 endpoint-only agent checks | initial agent/cores refusal before any workload; B4's watcher (reused `b4.Watch`/`watch_check`: agents, runner cores 4-7, every llama-server thread 4-7, detector threads 4-5, 30 s resolution) during the whole arm | `run_arm`; tests `Labels.test_invalid` (watch hit, agents, cores) |
| R1-6 P2 incomplete readings could pass | every reading (start, pause_start, pause_end, end) checked with `b4.reading_problems` (section rc, required fields, per-PID Pss/VmHWM); need = runner + llama_server, + robotcam while the camera is on; a missing start/end reading is INVALID | `need`, `arm_problems`; test `Labels.test_robotcam_memory_needed_while_the_camera_is_on` |
| R1-7 P2 thread scheduling not validated | no extra sampler (task: start/end + pause readings only); the watcher's cores 0-3 / nice 10 checked by `b4.watch_problems` -> `thread_problems` (reused) | `arm_problems` |
| R1-8 P2 tests without a clock | Mission tests use explicit times; mission-loop tests use B4's `FakeClock` advanced by detect and pauses; one real-time arm with every resource faked | `test_b5.py` |
| R1-9 P3 cleanup not independently protected | each cleanup action (scheduler join, watch, camera, heat control, server) through `b4.cleanup` (reused); a failing one never skips the others; carried-over interrupt limitation stated in README | `run_arm` finally |
| R2-1 P1 metadata before the stop could skip it | `b4.stop_camera` reused at every pause start and at cleanup (affinity read in its own try; stop always attempted; capture shown stopped within 10 s) | `mission_loop`, `run_arm` |
| R2-2 P1 restore crossing the deadline | B5's restore is part of a decision that `Mission.start` admitted before the deadline (checked before the restore); it counts in that decision's wall and in the overrun. No decision or restore can start after the deadline. Deliberate difference from B4: no restore-only row | `decide`, `Mission.start` |
| R2-3 P2 memory summed before validation | `b4.reading` / `mem_section` reused (per-PID `missing`) | `arm_problems` |
| R2-4 P2 only the server leader's affinity | `b4.watch_check` reused (every `/proc/<pid>/task` thread) | watcher |
| R3 vanished threads | reused (`vanished` counted, not a hit) | watcher |
| R3 P3 restore stats omit restore-only rows | not applicable (no restore-only rows); restores are inside decision rows (`restore` field, wall included) | `decide` |

Other B4-run notes carried into B5: stale RobotCam process (stop stops capture, not the process): RobotCam memory is
required only while the camera is on; server exit != 0 after SIGTERM reported as FINDING (#145), not a validity input.
END
